| **G-A4** Signal Anchor BasePart visual identity *(round-5: Relay cut — was "Anchor + Relay")* | **Deferred to `/asset-spec`** — see VA.6 follow-up #4. | deferred 2026-04-30 |

### VA.3 — Per-Moment Specifications

The 24 live moments (numbered 1–28; 16 + 17 retired round-2, 24 + 25 retired round-5, 26 + 27 added round-2, 28 added round-7). Cosmetic-boundary clearance applies to ALL: every cue is either HUD-non-skinnable or rendered on a scripted, non-skinnable child Part of the relevant BasePart. Cross-platform: all cues work on touch / mouse / gamepad (no hover-only states).

| # | Moment | Visual | Audio | HUD |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Bench-Armed | None at bench (avoids decorating lit-pool boundary per Principle 1) | Soft 2D proximity chime, < 200 ms, mid-range (1–2 kHz) | Interaction prompt fade-in 0.15s `Cubic/Out`; rectangular UIStroke 1px `#F0F0F0`, fill `#1A1E2B` @ 60% |

### VA.4 — Consolidation Families (effect re-use)

The 24 moments compose from 6 implementable effect families *(round-5: Moments 24/25 retired with the Relay cut; round-7: Moment 28 added to F1)*:

| Family | Implementation | Used by moments |
|---|---|---|
| **F1 — Craft Burst** | `craftBurst(tier, position, color)` — cool-white default, teal-white for tool events | 6, 9 (variant: upward column), 10, 28 (XS-tier, at the beacon position — round-7 in-window Canister use; position → beacon round-11 EC-3) |
| **F2 — Placement Ring** | `placementRing(diameter, position, color)` | 11 (Beacon), 20 (Anchor) |
| **F3 — Item Equip Pulse** | `equipPulse(item, player)` — per-item color + duration parameters | 15 (Coil) |
| **F4 — Waypoint Chevron** | Reuses art-bible §7.6 screen-edge marker system; color parameter distinguishes player ping (white) from tool event (teal-white) | 11, 20, 22 |
| **F5 — Expiry Fade** | `expireFade(element, duration, removeAfter)` | 14 (Beacon), 19 (Coil), 23 (Anchor) |
| **F6 — HUD Alert Text** | Reuses art-bible §7.3 alert sequence; `craftAlert(text, style)` parameter on existing alert system | 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 11, 13, 22, 27 |

*(Round-2 note: Moment 26 — the survival-window countdown — is a HUD timer/ring surface, not an alert-text or burst family; it reuses the art-bible §7.6 waypoint-marker + a countdown ring rather than a new effect family. Moment 27's victory beat reuses F6 for its alert + the HUD/art-bible §4.4 victory sequence.)*

### VA.5 — Audio System Contracts (forward obligations)

These are not implemented in this GDD — they are obligations on the Audio system / Audio GDD when authored:

1. **`OnBeaconActivated` audio mix transition**: ambient stem cuts; Hunt stem rises at window-start (Section B obligation). The Hunt stem sustains for the `BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW`, then resolves to a victory/return cue on `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` (round-2 — Moment 27) or is cut by the defeat path on a full-squad wipe (BCT-DEFEAT). Audio ADR will formalize the crossfade contract.
2. **Coil-modified Light pulse SFX variant**: softer Light sound when `EquippedEffects[playerId].dampenerCoil` is active. *(Round-2: the former Wrap-modified Sprint-pulse SFX variant was removed with the Quiet Step Wrap cut.)*
3. **Per-recipe completion SFX scaling**: each live tier (XS / MID / HEAVY) has a distinct sound family — players learn "loud vs. quiet" through both visual and audio. *(Round-5 reconciliation fix: the stale "RELAY" tier was removed from this list — the RELAY tier was retired round-3 and the Squad Relay recipe cut round-5; flagged round-4, corrected here.)*
4. **Bench ambient leak hum** (Moment 4): the spatial 3D loop with periodic harmonic shift matching the PointLight pulse.
5. **2D squad-broadcast tones** (Moments 5 join, 13 activation, 22 anchor trip — round-5: Moment 25 relay activation removed with the Relay cut): non-spatial tones heard by all alive squad members regardless of distance.

After the art bible is finalized, run `/asset-spec system:crafting-and-items` to generate per-asset visual specifications. Specific asset categories required:

1. **Bench BasePart hero asset** — material, color, form factor, no-emissive-at-rest constraint per G-A2
2. **Bench progress ring** — confirm HUD-anchored UIGradient (recommended) vs. world-space Neon ring
3. **Beacon BasePart hero asset** — silhouette read at predator-encounter distance per art-bible §2.7 column commitment
4. **Signal Anchor BasePart asset** — sensor read (not weapon/creature), teal-white emitter position
5. ~~**Squad Relay BasePart asset**~~ — **CUT (round-5, R4-2)** with the Squad Relay recipe; no asset required
6. **Per-item HUD badge icons** — `ui_icon_coil_32`, `ui_icon_anchor_32`, `ui_icon_beacon_objective_32` (32px canvas, 2px outline, no fill, art-bible §7.2 vocabulary). *(Round-2: `ui_icon_patch_32` + `ui_icon_wrap_32` dropped with the Composite Patch + Quiet Step Wrap cut. Round-5: `ui_icon_relay_32` dropped with the Squad Relay cut.)*
7. **Audio SFX asset specifications** — full sound asset specs deferred to Audio GDD authoring; this section names the design contracts
8. **Beacon activation white column + FogEnd** — environment/lighting system implementation; technical-artist confirms 80-stud PointLight vs. Neon Part geometry; iPhone SE mobile-downgrade variant needs profiling
9. **Audio stem crossfade contracts** — formalized in Audio ADR once Audio GDD is started
10. **Cosmetic asset checklist** — review-time gate verifying dual-clause compliance per H.67–H.72

> **📌 Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:crafting-and-items` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions, and generation prompts from this section.

## UI Requirements

This section catalogs the player-facing UI surfaces this system contributes. Visual treatment is locked in Visual/Audio (VA.1–VA.6); this section names the surfaces, their data bindings, and their interaction patterns. Detailed flow design (recipe-selection screen wireframe, multi-crafter join visualization, accessibility patterns) is deferred to `/ux-design crafting-bench` per the UX flag at UI.5.

### UI.1 — Surface Inventory

| # | Surface | Type | Visible to | Source moments | Data binding |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Bench Recipe Panel** | Modal panel (per-player) | Bench-Open through commit | VA.3 Moments 2–3, 5 | 4-row recipe list (round-7 reconciliation: 4 recipes post-round-5 Squad Relay cut; was "5-row" post-round-2); per-row affordability computed from squad combined personal pool; multi-crafter join state pre-selects active recipe |
| 2 | **Crafting Progress Ring (crafter)** | HUD overlay (own player) | BS2/BS3 active for this player | VA.3 Moment 4 | 48px UIGradient ring; lerps to server `craftProgress` at 5 Hz cadence (per H.61) |

| ~~10~~ | ~~**Relay Waypoint Chevron**~~ | — | — | — | **CUT (round-5, R4-2)** — surface number retired in place with the Squad Relay cut; not reused |
| 11 | **Alert Text Overlay** | Transient HUD banner | Squad-wide for major events; per-player for minor | VA.3 Moments 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 11, 13, 14, 19, 22, 27 (round-5: Moment 25 removed) | Reuses art-bible §7.3 alert sequence; `craftAlert(text, style)` with GothamBold 20px (urgent) / GothamMedium 16px (info) / GothamMedium 14px (subtle) |
| 12 | **Rejection Feedback** | Inline below recipe row | Per-player; bench panel must be open | VA.3 Moment 8 | Reason text from `OnCraftRejected.reason` enum; GothamMedium 14px; fades over 1.5s |
| 13 | **Beacon Survival-Window Countdown + Hold Indicator** | Persistent HUD timer/ring (squad-wide) **+ worldspace ground-ring** | Round-2 + round-3 B1 + round-5 lock | From `OnBeaconActivated` (BCT3) to window-end | VA.3 Moment 26 | `BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW ≈ 49 s` countdown ring/timer for all squad members; opened by `OnBeaconActivated`/`OnEscapeBeaconActivated`, cleared at window-end (victory, Moment 27) or defeat (wipe/scatter). The on-screen visual fallback that resolves the round-1 "Moment 13 audio-only" gap. **Hold-zone legibility — LOCKED round-5 (was /ux-design-deferred):** because scatter is a *lose* condition (E.27), the `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12 studs` hold zone is made legible **two ways, both required**: (1) a **worldspace ground-ring** at the beacon (Moment 26) showing the physical hold zone — clearest when a player is near the beacon; AND (2) a **HUD hold-state indicator** — a "HOLD THE BEACON" prompt + per-player in-radius / out-of-radius state, pushed live during BC4 by **`OnBeaconHoldStateChanged(playerId, inRadius)`** on each `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` boundary-cross (round-7 C-2 — the signal that carries the live per-member distance the round-6 panel found missing; presentation-only, does not change the window-end win evaluation) — which works when the beacon is off-screen or a player has scattered far (the exact case scatter-defeat punishes, where a world ring alone gives no cue). The two are redundant by design so a scatter-defeat is never an unsignalled loss. **Merged-chevron direction vector (round-9, Blocker #13):** when the Beacon waypoint chevron (surface 8) merges into this indicator during BC4, the merged element **carries the beacon-direction vector** (the screen-edge bearing toward `RunSession.beaconWorldPosition`) whenever the player is **out-of-radius** — so a scattered, off-screen player gets an explicit "the beacon is *this way*" directional cue, not merely a boolean "you are out." When the player is in-radius the directional chevron is hidden (they are already at the hold) and only the in-radius confirmation shows. The direction vector is the same waypoint math surface 8 already used; merging means one element, not two, during BC4. **Evaluative, not advisory (round-13 fail-fast — REVERSES the round-9 Blocker #10/Q3 advisory framing):** the hold is now **continuous and win-relevant** — the instant fewer than `requiredHolders = min(requiredHoldersBaseline, #aliveMembers)` (where `requiredHoldersBaseline = ceil(peakAliveCount/2)` is **frozen at BCT3** — round-16 R16-1 / round-19 R18-1; C.10 / H.118 — **NOT** the pre-round-16 live `ceil(#aliveMembers/2)` form that round-22 ux B-1 corrected here) alive members are within `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS`, the squad is in a **line-break** that forfeits the run within `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE ≈ 3 s` (C.9). So the HUD's hold state is authoritative about winning/losing, not merely advisory. The indicator therefore **leads with a squad-level HOLDING / BROKEN binary** computed from the **strict holder-threshold test** on `windowAliveInRadius` (`#windowAliveInRadius >= requiredHolders`, NO hysteresis): HOLDING whenever at least `requiredHolders` alive members are in radius, BROKEN the moment the count falls below — and while BROKEN it shows the **line-break grace countdown** (seconds remaining before scatter-defeat, derived server-side from `_lineBreakSince` and pushed on surface 13, C.9 step d). **The HOLDING/BROKEN states carry a colorblind shape/icon redundancy** (closed-ring vs broken-ring glyph, not color alone — ux F3, round-15), and the BROKEN→HOLDING (restore) transition is **server-debounced** (`BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE`, round-15) so a boundary-oscillating holder cannot flap the banner (H.95/H.119); BROKEN itself fires instantly (fail-fast alarm). This squad-level binary is the **authoritative win-state cue**; because it uses the strict radius with no hysteresis, it can never tell the squad they are HOLDING while the win check says otherwise — **dissolving the round-12 ux-B12-2 hysteresis trap** (where a 3-stud band could show a player "in-radius" at 32 studs while they actually lose). The per-member in/out chevrons (driven by `OnBeaconHoldStateChanged`, which keeps its `BEACON_HOLD_HYSTERESIS` band to debounce *individual-row* jitter) are demoted to **positional helpers** — "you specifically are outside; the beacon is *this way*" — and are explicitly NOT the win indicator; the squad banner is. **Movement *within* the 12-stud hold zone remains free; dropping the in-radius count below `requiredHolders` (round-15) is now a line-break, not a free excursion.** **Per-member row density (UX-B12-1 round-13):** on the iPhone-SE (375pt) viewport the squad-level HOLDING/BROKEN binary + grace countdown is the single always-present element; the per-member out-of-radius chevrons **collapse to a compact "N/M holding" count** (where `N` = members currently in radius and `M = requiredHolders` — the count the squad must keep `N >= M`, round-15) by default and expand to individual rows only above a width threshold `/ux-design` sets — so the ≤4-member case the round-12 panel flagged (UX-B12-1) can never stack four full hold-rows on the small screen; the squad binary carries the win-state and individual rows are progressive disclosure. **Line-break + window-end escalation (round-11 Conv #5, repurposed round-13):** under continuous-hold the urgency is carried primarily by the **BROKEN grace countdown** — the moment the line breaks, every out-of-radius member's indicator escalates to "REGROUP — Xs" carrying the live grace-remaining and the beacon direction-vector (the surface-8 bearing) made prominent. Additionally, in the window's final `BEACON_HOLD_ESCALATION_LEAD = 10 s` (G.6, presentation knob) a *holding* squad sees a calmer "HOLD — Xs to go" confirmation so the finale's end is legible. The escalation is **presentation-only** (it never changes the C.9 evaluation) and **client-derived (build-gate O5):** the client has the window-end from `OnBeaconActivated.windowDurationSeconds` (C.14 / Conv-2) and the per-member + grace state from `OnBeaconHoldStateChanged` + the surface-13 push — **no separate escalation server signal**. It must satisfy the WCAG ≤ 3 flashes/s cap (H.95) and the reduced-motion requirement (UI.5 item 6, H.111). Only the *visual styling* (ring thickness, color, HUD layout, the exact HOLDING/BROKEN + grace-countdown + escalation treatment, and the N/M-holding collapse threshold) goes to `/ux-design crafting-bench`; the world-ring-AND-HUD decision + the direction-vector + the **evaluative** semantics + the squad-level HOLDING/BROKEN binary + the grace countdown + the final-`BEACON_HOLD_ESCALATION_LEAD` escalation are locked here. **Denominator-change cue — REQUIRED (round-20 ux UX-B1):** the `M` in the `N/M holding` readout is `requiredHolders`, which **changes during the window** — a death lowers it (the `min(requiredHoldersBaseline, #aliveMembers)` clamp drops the bar), a respawn/reconnect raises it back toward (never above) the frozen `requiredHoldersBaseline` (E.33 / H.118). A silently shifting win-target in the highest-stakes 49 s reads as perceived unfairness — worst when a respawn raises `M` 1→2 and looks like the squad is being *punished* for a teammate returning. So **every change to `M` MUST carry a transient player-facing cue** (text + a non-color encoding per UI.4 — e.g. a brief "HOLDERS NEEDED: 2" flash with an up/down indicator) that names the new requirement **and its cause** ("a teammate fell — 1 holder needed now" / "a teammate is back — 2 holders needed"). The cue is **presentation-only** (it never changes the C.9 window evaluation) and is **client-derivable** — the client already has `#aliveMembers` (from the roster/alive signals) and `requiredHolders` (pushed on surface 13), so it fires on the `M`-change edge with no new server signal. It MUST satisfy the WCAG ≤ 3 flashes/s cap (H.95) and the reduced-motion requirement (UI.5 item 6). `/ux-design` owns the exact treatment; the requirement that an `M`-change is never silent is locked here, verified by H.130 |

### UI.2 — Interaction Patterns

| Surface | Touch (mobile) | Mouse (PC) | Gamepad |
|---|---|---|---|
| Bench Recipe Panel: navigation | Tap row to highlight | Click row to highlight | D-pad up/down or stick |
| Bench Recipe Panel: commit | **Two-tap pattern** (tap to highlight, tap again to commit — prevents accidental commits) | Single click on selected row OR Enter | A / X button |
| Bench Recipe Panel: cancel | Tap close button (44pt minimum target) OR back swipe | Esc key OR click outside panel | B / Circle button |
| Bench Recipe Panel: scroll (4 rows post-round-5 — fits iPhone SE without scroll in the default layout; `ScrollingFrame` retained only as an accessibility-text-scaling safety net) | Vertical scroll via `ScrollingFrame` | Mouse wheel | Right stick or D-pad |
| Item equip / use (HUD badge) | Tap badge | Number key bound per item | Bumper + face button |
| Signal Anchor: aim placement (round-5: Relay cut — Anchor is the only placeable) | Drag targeting cursor to desired position | Move cursor to position | Stick-aimed reticle |
| Signal Anchor: confirm placement | **Single tap** to confirm at aim point (low-consequence, repeatable) | Single click to confirm | A / X button |
| **Beacon: place** (B6 round-3) | **Tap-hold** `TAP_HOLD_COMMIT_DURATION = 0.5 s` at aim point (radial arc per art-bible §7.7) — high-consequence, irreversible | **Hold** click/`E` for 0.5 s | **Hold** A / X for 0.5 s |
| **Beacon: activate** (proximity-gated, B6 round-3) | **Tap-hold** 0.5 s on the activation prompt (radial arc) — opens the survival window, cannot be undone | **Hold** click/`E` for 0.5 s | **Hold** A / X for 0.5 s |

**No hover-only states**: every interactive surface has an explicit press / tap / button press. **Tap-hold commit (round-3 B6, beacon place + activate):** the hold is required on **all** input methods (touch tap-hold, PC hold-click/`E`, gamepad hold-A/X) — it is the friction model for the two irreversible beacon actions, so it cannot be touch-only. The *visual* differs by platform: the art-bible §7.7 **radial arc** renders on touch; on PC / gamepad the same 0.5 s hold is shown as a filling progress ring on the activation/placement prompt (so the player sees the commit charging and can release to cancel). **Release-to-cancel feedback (round-9, Blocker #13 — specified on all three platforms):** releasing before the 0.5 s commit aborts with no state change and an explicit cancel cue: on **touch**, the radial arc rapidly *unwinds* to empty (≈150 ms) and the prompt returns to its idle state; on **PC**, the filling ring drains to empty + the prompt label reverts (no audio sting — the action simply didn't happen); on **gamepad**, the ring drains to empty + a single short low-intensity rumble pulse (≤80 ms) marks the abort (distinct from the commit-success pulse, UI.4). In all three, an early release fires no RemoteEvent (the client only sends `RequestBeaconPlace` / `RequestBeaconActivate` once the hold completes), so a cancelled hold is server-side indistinguishable from no input. Verified by H.107. **Accessibility (round-19 ux nice-to-have):** the `TAP_HOLD_COMMIT_DURATION = 0.5 s` is a *client-side input-timing* value, so the client SHOULD honor the player's OS / Roblox hold-duration accessibility preference where one is exposed (lengthening the required hold for players who need it) — this changes only the local commit-charge timing, never the server contract (the server still validates the completed `RequestBeacon*` identically). `/ux-design` owns the exact mapping; the principle that the hold duration respects an OS accessibility setting is noted here. All other crafting commits stay single-tap (bench-open, Signal Anchor place — round-5: relay-activate removed) or two-tap (recipe-commit) and have no hold.

### UI.3 — Data Binding Contracts (Crafting → HUD)

The client-bound RemoteSignals declared at C.14 / C.15 drive these surfaces (round-2 added `OnBeaconActivated` window-start + `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` victory). HUD GDD MUST consume each:

| Signal (from C.14) | Drives surface(s) | Update cadence |
|---|---|---|
| `OnBenchArmed(benchId)` | Interaction prompt (Moment 1) | Event-driven |
| `OnBenchDisarmed(benchId)` | Interaction prompt fade-out | Event-driven |
| `OnCraftStarted(benchId, recipeId, crafters[])` | Progress ring init; bench panel close | Event-driven |
| `OnCraftProgress(benchId, progress)` | Progress ring lerp target | ≤ 5 Hz (per H.61) |
| `OnCraftCompleted(benchId, recipeId)` | Burst feedback; alert text; squad inventory icon flash | Event-driven |
| `OnCraftCancelled(benchId, recipeId, reason)` | Progress ring fade-out; reason-distinct alert text | Event-driven |
| `OnCraftRejected(reason)` | Rejection feedback inline | Event-driven |
| `OnInventoryChanged(scope, delta)` | Personal/squad inventory readouts (surfaces 6, 7) | Event-driven |
| `OnEquippedEffectExpired(playerId, itemId, reason)` | Equipped-effect badge fade; reason-distinct alert text | Event-driven |
| `OnSignalAnchorTripped(anchorId, anchorPosition)` | Squad-wide directional indicator (points to the Anchor) + alert text | Event-driven |
| `OnSignalAnchorExpired(anchorId, reason)` | Anchor waypoint marker fade; reason-distinct text | Event-driven |
| `OnBeaconPlaced(position, playerId)` | Beacon waypoint chevron creation; alert text | Event-driven |
| `OnBeaconActivated(position, activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` | **Activation CUE only (round-15 B-5):** the Moment-13 activation feedback — flora saturation, audio mix transition (C.12.1 audio), beacon-lit VFX. **Does NOT own the countdown** (that is `OnEscapeBeaconActivated`'s single consumer below). The `timestamp`/`windowDurationSeconds` fields are carried for parity (Conv-2) but the countdown surface binds to `OnEscapeBeaconActivated`. | Event-driven |
| `OnEscapeBeaconActivated(activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` | **Window-START countdown owner (round-15 B-5 — the missing UI.3 row):** opens the survival-window countdown (surface 13, Moment 26); the client computes window-end `= timestamp + windowDurationSeconds` to drive the countdown + the surface-13 escalation locally. **Single non-overlapping consumer:** `OnBeaconActivated` and `OnEscapeBeaconActivated` fire on the same BCT3 tick with the same `timestamp`/`windowDurationSeconds`, but the client acts on **exactly one each** — `OnBeaconActivated` → activation cue, `OnEscapeBeaconActivated` → countdown — so there is no double-open race (the round-14 B-5 ambiguity, where both rows "opened the countdown" and UI.3 listed only `OnBeaconActivated`). **NOT the victory trigger** (that is `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`). | Event-driven |
| `OnBeaconWindowSurvived(timestamp)` | Victory sequence (Moment 27); clears the survival-window countdown — round-2 | Event-driven |
| `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason, timestamp)` | Clears the survival-window countdown + hold indicator; PC's defeat screen takes over — scatter defeat, round-3 | Event-driven |
| `OnBeaconHoldStateChanged(playerId, inRadius)` | Per-member positional chevron on surface 13 (in-radius / out-of-radius + beacon direction-vector) during BC4 — round-7 C-2; round-13: a positional helper, NOT the win cue | Event-driven (boundary-cross, throttled) |
| `OnBeaconLineBreak(broken, timestamp, graceSeconds)` | Squad-level **HOLDING / BROKEN** binary + regroup countdown on surface 13 (round-13 — the authoritative win-state cue; client renders BROKEN + counts grace down from `timestamp + graceSeconds`). **Round-15: carries a colorblind shape/icon redundancy** (closed-ring HOLDING glyph vs broken-ring BROKEN glyph, not color alone — ux F3, mirroring surface-4) and the **`N/M holding`** readout where `M = requiredHolders` (now meaningful — the squad must keep `N >= M`). The `broken=false` restore edge is server-debounced (`BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE`) so the banner cannot flap (H.95/H.119). | Event-driven (squad-level HOLDING↔BROKEN edge, restore-debounced) |
| `OnBeaconDecayed(beaconId)` | Beacon waypoint marker fade (NOT fired on run-end per E.8, nor on window resolution per E.26) | Event-driven |

### UI.4 — Cross-Platform Requirements

- **Lowest-tier mobile target**: iPhone SE-class (375pt screen). All touch targets minimum 44pt. The 4-row recipe panel (round-7 reconciliation: 4 recipes post-round-5 Squad Relay cut; was 5 post-round-2, 7 pre-round-2) fits the iPhone SE viewport without scrolling in the default layout — directly relieving the round-1 "iPhone SE panel scrolling extends bench dwell" pillar-collision finding (with one fewer row than the round-2 fix, the headroom is larger); the `ScrollingFrame` is retained only as a safety net for accessibility text-scaling.
- **HUD safe area**: respect device notches and home-indicator zones on iOS/Android.
- **Gamepad rumble**: short pulse (100 ms, low intensity) on activating player's gamepad only for personal-confirmation moments (craft commit, beacon activate — round-5: relay activate removed) — not for squad-broadcast events. Mobile: no haptic beyond tap.
- **Colorblind safety**: HUD alerts use both color and text label (e.g., "ANCHOR TRIPPED" GothamBold 20px label backs the teal-white directional indicator). Squad-color crafter dots use shape variants in addition to color (per art-bible accessibility section if present).
- **Audio fallback**: 2D squad-broadcast tones (Moments 5, 13, 22) work on all platforms with no positional spatial-audio fallback required. *(Round-7: stale "Moment 25" removed — Relay activation was retired in the round-5 Squad Relay cut.)*

### UI.5 — UX Flag for `/ux-design`

> **📌 UX Flag — Crafting & Items**: This system has UI requirements covering bench recipe panel flow (4 rows post-round-5), multi-crafter join state visualization, equipped-effect badge layout (Coil only post-round-2), the Beacon survival-window countdown surface, and inventory readout density (squad pool with up to 4 distinct item types). In Phase 4 (Pre-Production), run `/ux-design crafting-bench` to create a UX spec for the bench recipe panel BEFORE writing epics. Stories that reference UI should cite `design/ux/crafting-bench.md`, not this section directly.
>
> Specifically, the following flows need detailed UX specification before implementation:
> 1. **Bench recipe panel** — recipe-selection flow with affordability + multi-crafter join state (4 rows post-round-5)
> 2. **Equipped-effect badge** — the single Coil badge with its 60 s countdown ring + final-10s warning. **Round-5: the final-10s warning must be non-color-only** (color shift paired with a shape/pulse change per UI.1 surface 4 / UI.4) — `/ux-design` owns the exact treatment but color-only is out of bounds.
> 3. **Squad inventory readout** — readability with 4 distinct item types in tight HUD real estate
> 4. **Beacon placement + activation tap-hold + survival-window countdown + hold indicator** — (round-3 B6) both beacon place and activate are **tap-hold** (`TAP_HOLD_COMMIT_DURATION = 0.5 s`); the hold-charge visual must read as a deliberate "ritual" commit (radial on touch, filling ring on PC/gamepad), never an accidental tap, and must be releasable-to-cancel before 0.5 s. The survival-window countdown must read as "survive this, then you win." **Round-5 LOCK (was a deferral):** the `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12 studs` hold (radius tightened 30→12 round-15 SENTRY fix; **round-16 stale-literal fix — this brief previously read `30 studs`**) is made legible **two ways, both required** — a **worldspace ground-ring** at the beacon (rendered at the true `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS`, read from config) AND a **HUD "HOLD THE BEACON" + in/out-of-radius state** (UI.1 surface 13) — so scatter-defeat (E.27, a *lose* condition) is never an unsignalled loss. `/ux-design` owns only the *styling*, not the world-ring-vs-HUD choice (that is locked to "both").
> 5. **Multi-crafter join visualization** — the "JOIN CRAFT" prompt + recipe pre-selected state when joining mid-craft
> 6. **Countdown-ring accessibility + activation-flora flash (round-5: WCAG now a hard requirement, not a deferral)** — reconcile the survival-window countdown ring with the art-bible's single-circle motif and provide a reduced-motion variant. **Reduced-motion is a HARD REQUIREMENT, not a styling nicety (round-11, ux UX-B2):** the three pulsing Crafting-owned BC4 surfaces — the survival-window countdown-ring pulse, the `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` world-ring pulse (Moment 26), and the Coil final-10s warning pulse (and the Conv #5 window-end escalation cue) — MUST each ship a **non-pulsing static variant** the client uses whenever the player's OS/Roblox reduced-motion preference is enabled (`UserGameSettings.ReducedMotion` / `GuiService` per the current Roblox API). The static variant conveys the same state (remaining time, in/out-of-radius, expiry-imminent) without animated pulsing. This is **distinct from** the WCAG ≤ 3-flash cap below — H.95 bounds flash *rate*; reduced-motion *removes* the pulse entirely when the player requests it (round-10 found H.95 alone covered only default timing, never affirmatively requiring a reduced-motion path). `/ux-design` owns only the exact static-variant treatment; the requirement that all three surfaces honor reduced-motion is locked here, verified by **H.111**. **WCAG 2.3.1 (≤ 3 flashes/second) split (round-7 C-3):** (i) the beacon-activation **flora** saturation/pulse (ED-owned flora subsystem, Moment 13) MUST meet ≤ 3 flashes/s — a **blocking accessibility obligation on ED's flora author** (F.4 ED row); AND (ii) **the Crafting-owned BC4 surfaces** — the survival-window countdown-ring pulse, the `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` world-ring pulse (Moment 26), and the Coil final-10s warning pulse — MUST themselves flash ≤ 3/s. This (ii) is the **Crafting-owned cap** the round-6 panel asked for (it rejected offloading the entire WCAG concern to the unwritten ED author with no Crafting-owned cap); Crafting owns the flash-rate of the surfaces Crafting renders, ED owns the flora. Verified by H.95. Neither is a `/ux-design` judgement call.
> 7. **BC4 HUD-density — RESOLVED with a hard surface budget (round-7 C-1, replaces the round-5/6 "deferred, blocking-for-implementation" non-closure).** During BC4 the Crafting-owned HUD contribution is **consolidated and capped at ≤ 4 concurrent surfaces**, via an explicit suppress-list: (a) **survival-window countdown + hold-state indicator** (surface 13) — always present; (b) a **compact aid-count strip** — the squad inventory readout (surface 7) collapses during BC4 to show only the consumable counts that matter in the window (primarily the Oxygen Canister stock); (c) the **equipped-effect badge** (surface 4) — present ONLY if a Coil is active; (d) the **alert overlay** (surface 11) — transient. **SUPPRESSED during BC4:** the personal-inventory readout (surface 6) and the bench progress rings (surfaces 2/3) — there is no gathering and no bench crafting at the beacon during the window, so these carry no actionable BC4 information; the **Coil Billboard worldspace indicator (surface 5)** — the owner's equipped-effect badge (surface 4) already conveys Coil state, so the squad-visible worldspace billboard is redundant noise during the hold (round-9, Blocker #12); the **Anchor waypoint chevron (surface 9)** — if an Anchor is still live at BC4 it is incidental to the hold, and its chevron competes with the beacon hold cue, so it is suppressed during BC4 and any Anchor *trip* during the window surfaces only through the transient alert overlay (surface 11) (round-9, Blocker #12); and the Beacon waypoint chevron (surface 8) **merges into** the hold-state indicator (surface 13 — see UI.1 surface 13 for the merged element's direction-vector semantics). `/ux-design crafting-bench` owns only the pixel layout of the ≤ 4 surfaces; the **surface budget and the full suppress-list (surfaces 2, 3, 5, 6, 8→13, 9) are locked here** (no new BC4 Crafting surface may be added without re-auditing density). Round-8 flagged surfaces 5 and 9 as breaching the cap (not previously on the list); they are now explicitly suppressed, restoring the ≤ 4 concurrent count. **Surface-11 multi-instance density (round-11, ux UX-B1):** the ≤ 4 cap counts surface 11 (the Alert Text Overlay) as *one surface ID*, but it is a banner that can render **multiple concurrent instances** — during BC4 several alerts can land near-simultaneously (Anchor trip, hold-state change, in-window Canister, decay) and stack past the iPhone SE (375pt) visual budget while the surface-count still reads "4." So during BC4 the overlay is **density-capped to at most 2 concurrent alert banners** (newest-on-top); a third+ coalesces into the most recent or queues ≤ 1 s rather than stacking — bounding *pixels*, not just surface IDs. `/ux-design crafting-bench` owns the exact stack/coalesce treatment; the ≤ 2-concurrent-banner BC4 cap is locked here. **Surface-13 internal density (round-13, UX-B12-1):** surface 13 counts as one surface ID against the ≤ 4 cap, but during a line-break it shows the squad-level HOLDING/BROKEN binary + grace countdown as the primary element, with per-member out-of-radius chevrons **collapsed to a compact "N/M holding" count** on the 375pt iPhone-SE viewport (expanding to individual rows only above a width threshold `/ux-design` sets) — so it can never stack four full hold-rows, closing the round-12 UX-B12-1 row-cap concern; see UI.1 surface 13. **Worst-case surface-13 pixel budget on 375pt — LOCKED (round-19 ux B-1, the still-missing-wireframe fix):** the worst-case BC4 surface-13 cluster — the HOLDING/BROKEN binary + grace countdown + the collapsed `N/M holding` count + the merged beacon-direction chevron — MUST fit a single stacked cluster within **≤ 25% of the iPhone-SE (375pt) safe-area height**, with **no per-member row expansion at 375pt** (rows expand only above the wider width threshold), leaving the rest of the ≤ 4-surface BC4 HUD (the aid-count strip, the Coil badge, the ≤ 2 alert banners) its share of the screen. `/ux-design crafting-bench` owes a **concrete 375pt wireframe + per-element pixel budget for this worst-case cluster as a required (non-deferred) deliverable** — the round-18 ux lens flagged its absence; the ≤ 25%-height cap and the no-row-expansion-at-375pt rule are **locked here**, only the exact pixels are `/ux-design`'s. Note the 4-row recipe panel (not shown during BC4) can still scroll at large accessibility text sizes (the `ScrollingFrame` safety net), which is acceptable.
>
> Update the systems index for Crafting & Items to flag this UX dependency.

## Acceptance Criteria

This section enumerates 122 live acceptance criteria (numbered through H.130; 8 retired — H.18/H.19/H.20/H.34/H.35/H.36/H.59 in the round-2 cut + H.23 in the round-5 Squad Relay cut; 8 added round-2 — H.73–H.80; 7 added round-3 — H.81–H.87; round-5: H.88–H.92 added; round-7: H.93–H.101 added; round-9: H.102–H.107 added — 6 ACs for the schema-first reconciliation; round-11: H.108–H.113 added — 6 ACs for the round-10 convergences + single-lens gaps + the build-from-artifact-gate findings; round-13: H.114–H.117 added — 4 ACs for the KITING fail-fast continuous-hold line-break + grace (H.114/H.115), the `windowDurationSeconds` payload propagation (H.116, Conv-2), and the Dampener interim-cap config guard (H.117, qa); round-15: H.118–H.121 added — holder-threshold respawn edge (H.118), restore-debounce flap (H.119), break-recover-win (H.120), mobile recoverability (H.121); **round-16: H.122–H.124 added — `_runEndCleanup` `_craftSessions` sweep (H.122), the `POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON` re-add predicate (H.123), the frozen `requiredHoldersBaseline` enumeration (H.124); H.118/H.119 revised — H.118 to the R16-1 freeze (respawn never raises the bar above the activation baseline), H.119 to the R16-2 `broken=true` edge-trigger**; **round-19: H.125–H.129 added — `graceSeconds=0` restore payload (H.125, R18-4), the post-MVP position-spoof velocity check forward obligation (H.126, R18-3), same-tick scatter-vs-wipe precedence (H.127), reconnect-during-BC4 (H.128), and the activate-late max-alive-so-far ratchet (H.129, R18-1); H.118/H.121/H.124 revised — H.118/H.124 to the R18-1 peak-source baseline, H.121 from an asserted radius-edge claim to the falsifiable joint mobile-recoverability inequality bound by the PA knockback cap**; **round-20: H.130 added — the denominator-change cue (ux UX-B1), so a mid-window change to `requiredHolders` is never a silent shift of the win-target**) validating Sections C, D, E, and F. Each AC is independently testable by a QA tester without reading the GDD. Format: GIVEN / WHEN / THEN + Test type (Logic / Integration / Visual-Feel / UI / Config-Data) + Source citation. **[P0]** marks BLOCKING (sprint-exit gate); unmarked entries are P1 (milestone gate); **[P2]** marks advisory. 85% of these ACs are tractable for automated testing per qa-lead review (2026-04-30); the 15% requiring playtest are noted in Test type.

> **Determinism preamble (round-3 B8/B9; round-5 R4-3 + qa-gate refinement):** every **time-dependent** AC below is evaluated against the **C.16 test-harness injection seam** — the test sets `_clock()` explicitly (and drives `_step` for the Heartbeat handler), so the assertions are **exact**, not wall-clock tolerances. **Round-5 (R4-3):** the D.1 progress is now a **clock-elapsed closed form** (`progressAtRateAnchor + ((_clock() − rateAnchorClock) × crafterSpeedMultiplier(N)) / CRAFT_BASE_DWELL_TIME`), NOT a per-tick accumulator and NOT `stepCount × dt` — so the H.27/H.28/H.30/H.31/H.41 assertions set `_clock()` to an exact dwell boundary and assert `craftProgress == 1.0` (or `0.5`) **exactly**: `10.0/10.0`, `5.0×2/10.0`, `2.0/2.0`, `2.5×2/10.0` are all exactly representable, so the result is bit-exact on any IEEE-754 runtime — no dependence on whether `600 × (1/60)` rounds to 10.0. This closes the round-4 IEEE-754 accumulation finding (the accumulator drifted over 300–600 additions) **and** the variable-`dt` incorrectness of a `stepCount × dt` form (real Heartbeat `dt` is jittery, so step-count×dt is not true elapsed time). The **bit-exact-equality** cluster (which asserts `craftProgress == 1.0`/`0.5` *exactly* at a representable dwell boundary) is **H.27, H.28, H.30, H.31, H.41, H.97** (round-23 systems B-2: H.97 is the BT4 rate-anchor-reset clock-boundary AC — `_clock() = startClock + 2.5 → craftProgress == 0.50` exact — structurally identical to the rest of this cluster; it was omitted at round-22 and is now appended). The broader **time-driven** cluster also includes H.46, H.61, H.73, H.76, H.77, the restart AC H.74, and **the round-13 line-break-grace ACs H.85/H.114/H.115 (the `_lineBreakSince` grace is compared against the injected `_clock`, exactly as the survival window is)**, **plus the round-15/19 grace/window ACs H.118, H.120, H.127, H.128 (each driven by the injected `_clock`/`_step` grace + window comparison — never a live clock or `task.wait`; the `_step`-edge-driven H.119/H.123/H.125/H.130 use the same harness)**, **plus the item-/sensor-lifetime + clock-guard ACs the round-21 sweep missed (round-22 qa B-1): H.22 (Signal Anchor `SIGNAL_ANCHOR_LIFETIME` expiry), H.37 (Dampener Coil `expiresAt` 60 s expiry), H.92 (window-end grace), H.89 (window-elapse + full-grace line-break — round-23 qa REC-1), H.102 (backward server-time-slew window guard), and H.129 (activate-late ratchet) — each compares an injected `_clock()` against a lifetime/expiry/grace/window value, never a `task.wait`/`os.time`** — all driven by the injected `_clock`/`_step`, none depending on the live clock, `task.wait`, an accumulator, or a fixed-`dt` assumption. **Forward-maintenance (round-21 qa B-1, the round-20-deferred fix): any new time-dependent AC MUST be appended to this enumeration — this list is the contract a test author reads to know "drive the seam, assert exact, never `task.wait`," so a stale list silently invites a wall-clock grace timer.** **H.39 (midpulse) is deliberately NOT in the bit-exact-equality cluster (round-7 D-3):** it is a `>=` **crossing test** (the midpulse fires on the first tick progress reaches-or-exceeds the threshold), which is robust to the float-representability of a non-default `CRAFT_BEACON_MIDPULSE_PROGRESS` in `[0.4, 0.6]` — so it does not (and must not) claim bit-exact equality at non-representable thresholds.

### H.1–H.26 — Core Rule ACs (per C.1–C.15)

**H.1 — Material count cap enforced on gather**
- **GIVEN** a player's `PersonalInventory.biomass = 20` (at `MATERIAL_STACK_MAX`)
- **WHEN** `OnGatherCompleted(playerId, "BIOMASS", 1)` fires from Resource Node
- **THEN** `PersonalInventory.biomass` remains 20; the incoming delta is silently discarded; no `OnInventoryChanged` fires for that player
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.4, G.2

**H.2 [P2] — Recipe listing: unaffordable recipes remain visible but dimmed**
- **GIVEN** a player opens the bench UI and the squad's combined personal pool cannot afford the Dampener Coil (requires 2 MINERAL, 1 RESONANT)
- **WHEN** the bench UI renders the recipe list
- **THEN** the Dampener Coil row is present in the list AND visually marked dimmed (not hidden or removed); all 4 recipes are always listed (round-5: Squad Relay cut). **Round-19 (ux nice-to-have — colorblind rule):** the unaffordable state must NOT be conveyed by dim/opacity alone (that violates the GDD's own UI.4 colorblind rule, which forbids color/opacity-only states) — the row also carries a **text deficit label** naming the missing material(s) (e.g. "Need 2 MINERAL"), so affordability reads without color/opacity discrimination.
- **Test type**: UI
- **Source**: C.3.3, UI.4 (colorblind rule)

**H.3 [P0] — BT1 fires only when player is alive and within range**
- **GIVEN** a player in PC.S4 (Dead-Respawning) fires `RequestCraft("OxygenCanister")` from any position
- **WHEN** the server validates the request
- **THEN** the server rejects with `OnCraftRejected(reason="not-alive")` and no `CraftSession` is created
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.4, C.15, E.20

**H.4 [P0] — BT1 proximity gate: rejection beyond bench range**

- **WHEN** the player fires `RequestCraft("OxygenCanister")`
- **THEN** the server rejects with `OnCraftRejected(reason="out-of-range")` and no `CraftSession` is created
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.1, C.15

**H.5 — Bench state machine: BS1 → BS2 → BS3 on valid join**
- **GIVEN** bench is in BS2 with `craftProgress = 0.50` and a second player within range fires `RequestCraft` for the same recipe
- **WHEN** the server validates the join (progress < `CRAFT_JOIN_CUTOFF = 0.85`; bench has room)
- **THEN** bench transitions to BS3; `CraftSession.crafters` array has length 2; `OnBenchOccupancyChanged(benchId, 2)` fires to all squad clients
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.5, C.8 BT2

**H.6 — BT2 join cutoff: strict less-than at 0.85**
- **GIVEN** bench is in BS2 with `craftProgress = 0.85` exactly
- **WHEN** a second player fires `RequestCraft` for the same recipe
- **THEN** the server rejects with `OnCraftRejected(reason="too-late-to-join")`; bench remains in BS2
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.5, C.8, E.3

**H.7 [P0] — BT3 atomic material deduction and item grant**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS2 with `craftProgress` about to reach 1.0; squad has exactly the required materials for Oxygen Canister (2 BIOMASS, in the crafting player's personal inventory)
- **WHEN** `craftProgress >= 1.0` on a Heartbeat tick
- **THEN** in a single atomic operation: (1) 2 BIOMASS deducted from personal inventory; (2) one Oxygen Canister added to `SquadInventory`; (3) `DisturbanceService:Emit("Craft", benchPosition, 0.10, crafterId)` called; (4) `OnCraftCompleted` fires to squad; (5) `CraftSession` cleared; (6) bench returns to BS1 — all within the same server Heartbeat
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.6, C.8 BT3, D.3

**H.8 [P0] — BT5: no partial credit on full cancel**

- **WHEN** the sole crafter fires `RequestCraftCancel`
- **THEN** `craftProgress` is discarded (reset to 0.0 internally; `CraftSession` cleared); no materials are deducted; no item is added; no `DisturbanceService:Emit` is called; `OnCraftCancelled(benchId, recipeId, reason="player-initiated")` fires to squad

**H.9 [P0] — Dead player inventory lock: not cleared, not poolable**

- **THEN** the dead player's personal inventory values are excluded from the combined-pool calculation; `PersonalInventory` for the dead player remains {5, 3, 2} throughout the death state
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.6, C.7, E.7

**H.10 — Inventory unlocks on respawn**

**H.13 [P0] — BCT2: beacon placed, removed from inventory, placement flags set**

**H.14 [P0] — BCT3: activation fires emission with placer attribution**

**H.15 [P0] — BCT3 fires the window-START signal, NOT victory (round-2)**

This part is fully deterministic and IS the sprint-exit gate for this AC.

This **cannot pass until PC's subscription is authored** (PC in MAJOR REVISION round-6; F.4/F.6) and is NOT a sprint-exit blocker for the Crafting sprint.

### H.27–H.41 — Formula ACs (per D.1–D.7)

**H.27 — D.1: craft progress is exact at N=1 (Oxygen Canister), clock-boundary**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS2 with a single crafter and recipe `OxygenCanister` (craft time 2 s); at BT1 `CraftSession.rateAnchorClock = startClock` and `progressAtRateAnchor = 0.0` (C.16 seam controls `_clock()`; no live clock, no `task.wait`)
- **WHEN** the test sets `_clock() = startClock + 1.0` then `_step` (mid-craft), and separately `_clock() = startClock + 2.0` then `_step` (the 2 s dwell)
- **THEN** at `+1.0`: `craftProgress == (1.0 × 1)/2.0 == 0.5` **exactly**; at `+2.0`: `craftProgress == (2.0 × 1)/2.0 == 1.0` **exactly** and BT3 fires. Both boundaries (0.5, 1.0) are exactly representable in IEEE-754 — **no tolerance**, bit-exact on any runtime (D.1 is `(_clock() − rateAnchorClock) × mult / dwell`, evaluated by the division-last form, not a precomputed reciprocal)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.1, C.16

**H.28 — D.1: craft progress is exact at N=1 (Escape Beacon), clock-boundary**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS2, single crafter, recipe `EscapeBeacon` (craft time 10 s), `progressAtRateAnchor = 0.0`, `rateAnchorClock = startClock` (C.16 seam)
- **WHEN** the test sets `_clock() = startClock + 10.0` and calls `_step`
- **THEN** `craftProgress == (10.0 × 1)/10.0 == 1.0` **exactly** and BT3 has fired exactly once; a `_step` at `_clock() = startClock + 9.9999` first yields `craftProgress < 1.0` (not yet complete). `10.0/10.0` is exactly 1.0 — no dependence on whether `600 × (1/60)` rounds to 10.0 (round-5 qa-gate fix)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.1, C.16

**H.29 [P0] — D.1: progress clamped at 1.0, does not overflow**
- **GIVEN** a craft where `_clock() = rateAnchorClock + 10.0017` (an elapsed time that would compute `craftProgress = 1.00017` before clamping)
- **WHEN** the D.1 closed form evaluates
- **THEN** `craftProgress` is clamped to exactly 1.0 (not 1.00017); BT3 fires; no second BT3 fires on the subsequent tick (the session is cleared at BT3)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.1

**H.30 — D.2: N=2 halves effective dwell time, clock-boundary**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS3 with 2 crafters on Escape Beacon (10 s base dwell); `progressAtRateAnchor = 0.0`, `rateAnchorClock = startClock` (C.16 seam)
- **WHEN** the test sets `_clock() = startClock + 5.0` and calls `_step`
- **THEN** `craftProgress == (5.0 × 2)/10.0 == 1.0` **exactly** and BT3 has fired — completion in half the wall-clock of solo (10 s). `5.0 × 2 / 10.0` is exactly 1.0 — bit-exact, no tolerance
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.2, C.16

**H.31 — D.2: progress preserved when N changes mid-craft, clock-boundary**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS2 on a 10 s Beacon; `rateAnchorClock = startClock`; the test sets `_clock() = startClock + 5.0` and `_step` to reach `craftProgress == (5.0)/10.0 == 0.50` **exactly** (N=1)
- **WHEN** a second crafter joins (BT2 — the handler captures `progressAtRateAnchor = 0.50` and `rateAnchorClock = joinClock`), then the test sets `_clock() = joinClock + 2.5` and `_step` (N=2 rate)
- **THEN** `craftProgress == 0.50 + (2.5 × 2)/10.0 == 0.50 + 0.50 == 1.0` **exactly**; BT3 fires; the 0.50 captured at the join was preserved (the closed form continues from `progressAtRateAnchor`, it does not discard or recompute-from-zero). All three boundary values (0.50, 0.50, 1.0) are exactly representable — bit-exact, no tolerance, and the result is independent of the per-frame `dt` (it depends only on the elapsed `_clock` interval)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.2, C.16

**H.32 [P0] — D.3: two independent Emit calls fire at N=2 completion**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS3 with crafters A and B completing Escape Beacon (`craftProgress` reaches 1.0)
- **WHEN** BT3 fires
- **THEN** `DisturbanceService:Emit` is called exactly twice — once with `sourcePlayerId = A.UserId`, once with `sourcePlayerId = B.UserId` — both at `benchPosition`, both with `initialMagnitude = MAGNITUDE_CRAFT_HEAVY = 0.35`; the two calls are independent (not merged)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.3, C.11.1

**H.33 — D.3: N=2 bench-position field does not breach reserved band**
- **GIVEN** two crafters complete Escape Beacon simultaneously (D.3 fires two Emit calls at 0.35 each)
- **WHEN** `fieldValue(benchPosition)` is queried immediately after both Emit calls
- **THEN** `fieldValue <= 1.0` (clamped) AND no individual emission had `initialMagnitude > 0.85` (reserved-band constraint per ED.C.1.2); the two 0.35 emissions are independently below 0.85
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.3, C.11.5

**H.34, H.35, H.36 — *REMOVED (round-2, 2026-06-01)*** — all three tested cut-recipe formulas: H.34 + H.35 tested the Composite Patch gather-magnitude downgrade (D.4, Heavy→Medium and the Light floor case), H.36 tested the Quiet Step Wrap sprint-pulse reduction (D.5). D.4 and D.5 are themselves removed (round-2 stubs); these ACs have no referent. Numbers retired (not reused). The only surviving emission modifier — the Dampener Coil — is tested by H.37 (Light-pulse reduction) and H.38 (the canonical Coil × stationary order-of-operations floor-discard, D.6a).

**H.37 — D.6: Dampener Coil reduces Light pulse to 0.04 for 60 s**
- **GIVEN** a player equips Dampener Coil at injected clock `T = _clock()` (the **C.16 `_clock`/`_step` seam** drives all timing — the test sets `_clock()` to `T+4, T+8, …` and `_step`s rather than waiting wall-clock seconds, per the determinism preamble); 5 Light pulse emissions fire at `T+4, T+8, T+12, T+16, T+20` (each at 4 s intervals per `LIGHT_PULSE_INTERVAL`)
- **WHEN** each emission fires during the Coil's active window (`DAMPENER_DURATION = 60 s`, the `expiresAt = T + 60` comparison evaluated against the injected `_clock()`)
- **THEN** each of the 5 calls to `DisturbanceService:Emit` carries `initialMagnitude = 0.08 × 0.50 = 0.04`; the Coil is NOT consumed per pulse; at injected `_clock() = T+60` the Coil expires and the next pulse fires at 0.08
- **Test type**: Logic (time-dependent assertion driven by the injected `_clock`, not the live clock)
- **Source**: D.6, C.16

**H.38 [P2] — D.6: stationary Coil-equipped player's effective pulse falls below MAGNITUDE_FLOOR**
- **GIVEN** a player equips Dampener Coil and is stationary (`movement_delta < EMITTER_MOVEMENT_DELTA_FLOOR`)
- **WHEN** a Light pulse fires (Coil applies 0.50 reduction to nominal magnitude; PC.D.4 then applies `STATIONARY_EMISSION_FACTOR = 0.30`)
- **THEN** the chained reduction yields `0.08 × 0.50 × 0.30 = 0.012`, which is below `MAGNITUDE_FLOOR = 0.02`; ED's schema validation discards the emission; `DisturbanceService:Emit` is NOT called; the Coil is NOT consumed for this pulse
- **Test type**: Logic — **but the Stage-2 half is unverified-until-PC (round-7 A-5):** the Coil's Stage-1 ×0.50 factor is Crafting-owned and testable now; the chained product with PC's Stage-2 `STATIONARY_EMISSION_FACTOR` requires PC.D.4 to apply the canonical D.6a chain (the pulse owner applies both factors once — A-5 ownership ruling), so the full `0.08 × 0.50 × 0.30` product is verified end-to-end only when PC's revision lands (PC in MAJOR REVISION round-6).
- **Source**: D.6, D.6a (Stage ownership), PC.D.4, ED zero-magnitude rejection

**H.39 — D.7: Beacon mid-craft pulse fires exactly once per craft session (crossing test, round-7 D-3)**
- **GIVEN** a Beacon craft is in progress at N=1 with `craftProgress` advancing from 0 to 1.0 (C.16 seam drives `_clock`)
- **WHEN** the first `_step` on which `craftProgress >= CRAFT_BEACON_MIDPULSE_PROGRESS` is evaluated (at any threshold in the tunable range `[0.4, 0.6]`)
- **THEN** `DisturbanceService:Emit("Craft", CraftSession.benchPosition, MAGNITUDE_CRAFT_BEACON_MIDPULSE=0.18, CraftSession.primaryCrafterId)` fires exactly once (attribution uses the stable `primaryCrafterId` field per round-2 B7, NOT `crafters[1]`); `CraftSession.midpulseFired = true`; subsequent ticks do not re-fire the midpulse in the same session
- **THEN (boundary contract — round-7 D-3):** this is a **`>=` CROSSING test, NOT an exact-equality assertion** — the midpulse fires on the first tick where progress reaches-or-exceeds the threshold, so it is **robust to the float-representability of the threshold**. The round-6 panel flagged that "bit-exact" wording held only at the default `0.5` (a representable value) and broke for non-representable thresholds in `[0.4, 0.6]` (e.g. `0.4` is not exactly representable). A crossing test has no such dependence: whatever the closest double to the threshold is, the first tick that reaches-or-exceeds it fires the midpulse exactly once. To make the test itself deterministic, the test sets `_clock` so the computed `craftProgress` lands at-or-just-past the threshold (at the default `0.5`, `_clock = rateAnchorClock + 5.0` gives exactly `0.5`; for a non-default threshold the test advances to the first `_step` past it). This AC therefore does **not** belong to the bit-exact-equality cluster (H.27/28/30/31/41) — it is a once-only crossing assertion.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.7, C.16

**H.40 — D.7: midpulse does not fire if Beacon craft cancelled before threshold**
- **GIVEN** a Beacon craft is in progress and `craftProgress = 0.40` (below `CRAFT_BEACON_MIDPULSE_PROGRESS = 0.5`)
- **WHEN** the crafter fires `RequestCraftCancel` (BT5 fires)
- **THEN** `DisturbanceService:Emit` has NOT been called for the midpulse at any point during this session; `CraftSession.midpulseFired` was never set to `true` before clearing
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.7

**H.41 — D.7: N=2 midpulse at 2.5 s wall-clock with primary-crafter attribution, clock-boundary**
- **GIVEN** Beacon craft starts at N=2 (two crafters already joined at BT1/BT2); `rateAnchorClock = startClock` (C.16 seam)
- **WHEN** the test sets `_clock() = startClock + 2.5` and calls `_step` (2.5 s wall-clock at N=2 on a 10 s Beacon)
- **THEN** `craftProgress == (2.5 × 2)/10.0 == 0.50` **exactly** and the midpulse fired on this `_step` (it crossed `CRAFT_BEACON_MIDPULSE_PROGRESS = 0.5`; `midpulseFired = true`); the midpulse uses `sourcePlayerId = CraftSession.primaryCrafterId` (the stable BT1-initiator field — round-2 B7; NOT `crafters[1]`, which can shift if the initiator departs via BT4, and NOT crafters[2]). `2.5 × 2 / 10.0` is exactly 0.50 — bit-exact, no tolerance
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.7, C.16

### H.42–H.55 — Edge Case ACs (8 P0 + selected P1 from E)

**H.42 [P0] — E.6: mid-craft material race: BT6 fires, no partial deduction**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS2 crafting Dampener Coil (requires 2 MINERAL, 1 RESONANT); at BT1 the combined pool had sufficient materials; a concurrent BT3 on another bench deducts those materials before this bench reaches completion
- **WHEN** this bench's `craftProgress >= 1.0` and BT3's atomic deduction is attempted
- **THEN** the deduction fails; BT6 fires: no materials deducted, no item added to `SquadInventory`, no `DisturbanceService:Emit` called; `OnCraftCancelled(benchId, recipeId, reason="insufficient-materials")` fires to squad; bench returns to BS1
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.6, C.8 BT6, E.6

**H.43 [P0] — E.7: solo crafter dies; inventory locked, not wiped**
- **GIVEN** player P is the sole crafter in BS2 with all Beacon materials (BIOMASS=2, MINERAL=3, RESONANT=3) in personal inventory; `craftProgress = 0.70`
- **WHEN** `Humanoid.Died` fires for player P
- **THEN** BT5 fires (session cleared, progress discarded); `PersonalInventory[P]` values remain {2, 3, 3} (not cleared); a concurrent `RequestCraft` from another bench's BT3 combined-pool check CANNOT include P's locked inventory; `PersonalInventory[P]` becomes accessible again only after PC's respawn event fires
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.6, C.7, E.7

**H.44 [P0] — E.9: activator dies on same Heartbeat as activation; activation still succeeds (round-2)**
- **GIVEN** a player fires `RequestBeaconActivate` and `Humanoid.Died` fires for that player on the same Heartbeat; the activate request is processed first (Luau single-threaded ordering); at least one other squad member remains alive
- **WHEN** BCT3 validates (alive check passes; `beaconActivated == false`)
- **THEN** BCT3 fires atomically: `RunSession.beaconActivated = true`; `DisturbanceService:Emit("Beacon", beaconWorldPosition, 0.95, beaconPlacerId)` called; `OnEscapeBeaconActivated` fires (window-start); the player's subsequent death does NOT rollback the activation and does NOT end the run. **The survival window opens; victory is NOT awarded at activation** — it is awarded only if at least `requiredHolders` squad members are alive AND within `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12 studs` at window-end (BCT4, holder-threshold round-15, H.73). The activator's death merely removes one survivor from the window; the run continues. *(Round-2: pre-patch this AC asserted "the run ends in victory" at activation — corrected to window-survival semantics.)*
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.9, E.9, C.5.6a

**H.45 [P0] — E.12: both crafters die simultaneously; no zombie CraftSession**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS3 with crafters A and B; both `Humanoid.Died` events fire on the same Heartbeat
- **WHEN** the death handlers process sequentially (Luau scheduler orders A first, then B)
- **THEN** A's handler fires BT4 (bench → BS2 with B still in crafters, but B is also dead); B's handler detects `crafters` array would be empty after removal and fires BT5 instead of BT4; `CraftSession` is cleared; no emission; bench is in BS1 with no orphaned session
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.8, E.12

**H.46 [P0] — E.13: server-side proximity watcher cancels craft on range violation**
- **GIVEN** a player is in BS2 crafting and walks to `GATHER_PROXIMITY_RADIUS + 2 studs = 6 studs` from the bench WITHOUT sending `RequestCraftCancel`
- **WHEN** the server-side proximity watcher fires on its next `FIELD_UPDATE_HZ = 5` tick (driven by the C.16 `_step` seam — the test advances to the next watcher tick rather than waiting 0.2 s of wall-clock)
- **THEN** on that watcher tick (≤ 1 `FIELD_UPDATE_HZ` interval, i.e. ≤ 0.2 s-equivalent, after the violation), the watcher fires BT5: `CraftSession` cleared, progress discarded, no emission, `OnCraftCancelled(benchId, recipeId, reason="out-of-range")` fires to squad; the check is server-authoritative (client-reported position is NOT used for this determination)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.8, E.13, G.5, C.16

**H.47 [P0] — E.20: item-use before BT3 fires; rejected**
- **GIVEN** a player fires `RequestUseItem("OxygenCanister")` before the server's BT3 has added the item to `SquadInventory`
- **WHEN** the server processes the request
- **THEN** `SquadInventory["OxygenCanister"].count = 0` check fails; request rejected with `OnCraftRejected(reason="item-unavailable")`; no oxygen pulse is fired; `DisturbanceService` is not called
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.6, C.15, E.20

**H.48 [P0] — E.21: inventory inflation attack rejected at schema level**
- **GIVEN** a malicious client fires `RequestCraft("EscapeBeacon")` when the server's `PersonalInventory[playerId]` has RESONANT=0 (below the 3 needed)
- **WHEN** BT1 validation reads the server-authoritative inventory
- **THEN** the combined-pool check fails (RESONANT is 0 on server); request rejected with `OnCraftRejected(reason="insufficient-materials")`; the `RequestCraft` payload contains only `recipeId` — no material-count field is present for the client to manipulate
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.15, E.21

**H.49 [P0] — E.22: false victory via RequestBeaconActivate before placement**
- **GIVEN** `RunSession.beaconPlaced = false` (Beacon not yet placed)
- **WHEN** a player fires `RequestBeaconActivate`
- **THEN** BCT3 pre-validation checks `beaconPlaced == true` and fails; request rejected with `OnBeaconRejected(reason="beacon-not-placed")`; no `DisturbanceService:Emit` called; no `OnEscapeBeaconActivated` fires; `RunSession.beaconActivated` remains `false`
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.15, E.22

**H.50 — E.5: deserter exploit closed; emission at BT3 uses current crafters array**
- **GIVEN** bench in BS3 with `craftProgress = 0.90`; crafter[1] fires `RequestCraftCancel`
- **WHEN** BT4 removes crafter[1] and crafter[2] later reaches BT3
- **THEN** D.3 iterates `CraftSession.crafters` at the moment of BT3, which contains only crafter[2]; exactly one `DisturbanceService:Emit` fires (attributed to crafter[2]); crafter[1] receives no attribution
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.8, D.3, E.5

**H.51 — E.16: second Signal Anchor placement cancels first**
- **GIVEN** a live Signal Anchor is deployed (within its `SIGNAL_ANCHOR_LIFETIME = 45 s`); a player places a second Signal Anchor
- **WHEN** the server validates the second placement request
- **THEN** the first Anchor's BasePart is removed; `OnSignalAnchorExpired(firstAnchorId, reason="replaced")` fires to squad; the second Anchor BasePart spawns; `ActiveDeployables.signalAnchor` points to the second Anchor; only one Signal Anchor exists in the world
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.6, C.10, E.16

**H.52 — E.17 / H.119: in-window Craft emits at the BEACON position (round-15 Ruling 3)**
- **GIVEN** three variants via the C.16 seam — (a) Beacon in a LIVE survival window (`beaconActivated == true and not runOutcomeResolved`), a craft completes at a bench more than `INFLUENCE_RADIUS = 24 studs` from the Beacon; (b) the same craft completes while the beacon is NOT in a live window (pre-activation, `beaconActivated == false`); (c) the craft completes AFTER the window has resolved (`runOutcomeResolved == true` — covering BOTH victory [`beaconWindowSurvived == true`] and scatter/wipe defeat [`beaconWindowSurvived == false`], in the window between the terminal tick and the `_craftSessions` cleanup sweep, H.122)
- **WHEN** BT3 fires and computes `emitPosition` (C.8 step 3)
- **THEN** in variant (a) `DisturbanceService:Emit("Craft", RunSession.beaconWorldPosition, recipeBurstMagnitude, crafterId)` is called — the burst lands at the **beacon** position (NOT `benchPosition`) and is therefore subject to ED.C.3.6's Hunt-floor cap at that position like any beacon-position emission; in variant (b) the burst is emitted at `benchPosition` as normal; **in variant (c) the burst is emitted at `benchPosition` (NOT the stale `beaconWorldPosition`) — a post-resolution craft never draws to the beacon position in an ended run (round-23 qa BLOCKING-1; mirrors H.93(c) for the in-window Canister path)**. The asserted distinguisher is the `position` argument equals `beaconWorldPosition` **iff the survival window is live** (`beaconActivated == true and not runOutcomeResolved`), and `benchPosition` otherwise (both pre-activation variant (b) and post-resolution variant (c)).
- **Test type**: Integration
- **Source**: E.17, C.8 BT3, E.32, ED.C.3.6

**H.53 — E.18: N=2 emissions at same position: two independent sources in ED live-source list**
- **GIVEN** D.3 fires two Emit calls at BT3 (same `benchPosition`, same `initialMagnitude`, different `sourcePlayerId`)
- **WHEN** ED processes both emissions
- **THEN** two distinct source entries exist in the live-source list with distinct `emissionId`s; they do NOT merge into one entry; both decay independently; `sourcePlayerId` is distinct for each entry
- **Test type**: Integration
- **Source**: D.3, E.18, ED.C.1.5

**H.54 [P0] — Once-per-run flags never reset within a RunSession**
- **GIVEN** `RunSession.beaconCrafted` has been set to `true` at BCT1
- **WHEN** any server-side code path is executed before `RunEnded` fires (including beacon placement, activation, or decay)
- **THEN** `RunSession.beaconCrafted` remains `true`; similarly `RunSession.beaconPlaced` remains `true` after BCT2 and `RunSession.beaconActivated` remains `true` after BCT3; none of these flags are ever set back to `false` within the same RunSession
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.5.1, C.9, E.10

**H.55 [P0] — Equipped effect death cleanup: same-Heartbeat atomicity (round-2: Coil-only)**
- **GIVEN** a player with an active Dampener Coil (the only equippable post-round-2) dies (PC.T5 fires)
- **WHEN** `Humanoid.Died` is processed on the server
- **THEN** on the SAME Heartbeat as the death event: `EquippedEffects[playerId].dampenerCoil` is cleared; `OnEquippedEffectExpired(playerId, "DampenerCoil", reason="death")` fires; the Coil is NOT returned to SquadInventory (it was consumed on equip — E.1); no pending input from that tick can read a stale equipped-state for the dead player
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.10, C.6, E.1, G2 gap-resolution
- *(Round-2: pre-patch this AC also covered a Composite Patch death-return path; with the Patch cut, only the Coil's consume-on-equip semantic remains.)*

### H.56–H.62 — Cross-System Contract ACs (per F.1–F.4)

**H.56 [P0] — F.1/F.2: OnEscapeBeaconActivated fires exactly once per RunSession**
- **GIVEN** BCT3 fires (beacon activated)
- **WHEN** `OnEscapeBeaconActivated(activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` is fired
- **THEN** the signal fires to the squad channel exactly once; if BCT3 is somehow invoked a second time (defended against by `beaconActivated` guard), `OnEscapeBeaconActivated` does NOT fire a second time within the same RunSession
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.5.6, C.9 BCT3, C.12.1, F.2

**H.57 — F.2: RequestInteract routing to correct Crafting handler**
- **GIVEN** Player Controller fires `RequestInteract(targetId)` where `targetId` resolves to a crafting bench
- **WHEN** the Crafting Service's interaction router processes the request
- **THEN** the request is routed to the bench-open handler (equivalent to BT1 prerequisites); if `targetId` resolves to nothing owned by Crafting, `InteractRejected(reason="not-crafting-target")` is returned; PC may then try other systems
- **Test type**: Integration
- **Source**: C.12.2, F.2

**H.58 — F.2/C.13: OnGatherCompleted adds to PersonalInventory, capped**
- **GIVEN** `PersonalInventory[playerId].mineral = 18` and RN fires `OnGatherCompleted(playerId, "MINERAL", 3)`
- **WHEN** Crafting Service processes the signal
- **THEN** `PersonalInventory[playerId].mineral = 20` (capped at `MATERIAL_STACK_MAX`); the extra 1 unit is silently discarded; `OnInventoryChanged` fires to the owning client with delta reflecting the actual change (2, not 3)
- **Test type**: Integration
- **Source**: C.4, C.13, F.2

**H.59 — *REMOVED (round-2, 2026-06-01)*** — tested the Composite Patch ↔ Resource Node `GetEquippedEffect` accessor (the gather-magnitude-downgrade read). With the Patch cut, RN no longer reads any Crafting-side equipped-effect state before computing gather emission, and Crafting exposes no such accessor — a net simplification of the RN contract (C.13). Number retired (not reused).

**H.60 — F.2/C.14: HUD receives OnInventoryChanged on every squad inventory mutation**
- **GIVEN** a craft completes (BT3 fires) and adds one Oxygen Canister to `SquadInventory`
- **WHEN** BT3's atomic step 4 fires `OnInventoryChanged`
- **THEN** `OnInventoryChanged({scope="squad", itemId="OxygenCanister", newCount=<N+1>, delta=+1})` is pushed to all alive squad clients; the signal fires simultaneously with `OnCraftCompleted` on the same tick
- **Test type**: Integration
- **Source**: C.14, F.2

**H.61 — F.2/C.14: OnCraftProgressUpdate push throttled to ≤ 5 Hz (server-side count, injected clock)**
- **GIVEN** bench is in BS2 with an active CraftSession; the test drives 60 `_step(1/60)` calls with `_clock` advancing by 1/60 each, modelling 1 s of 60 Hz server tick (C.16 seam)
- **WHEN** the server accumulates `craftProgress` across those 60 steps and the push-throttle (driven by `_clock()`) decides when to emit `OnCraftProgressUpdate`
- **THEN** the **server emits at most 5** `OnCraftProgressUpdate` signals across the simulated 1 s (asserted on the server-side emit count from the throttle — NOT a client receive count, which would be non-deterministic over the network); the server's canonical `craftProgress` continues to accumulate every step (all 60) regardless of the push rate. *(Round-3 B8: re-specified as a deterministic server-side throttle assertion against the injected clock; the prior "client receives at most 5" was a non-deterministic client-side count.)*
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.14, C.15, C.16

**H.62 — F.2/C.13: RN must fire OnGatherCompleted AFTER its own Gather Emit**
- **GIVEN** a gather event fires from Resource Node
- **WHEN** RN's gather completion sequence executes
- **THEN** `DisturbanceService:Emit("Gather", nodePosition, magnitude, playerId)` fires BEFORE `OnGatherCompleted` is signaled; Crafting's inventory update cannot precede the disturbance emission (ordering per ED.C.3.1)
- **Test type**: Integration
- **Source**: C.13, F.4 (RN obligation)

### H.63–H.66 — Performance & Budget ACs

**H.63 [P2] — Server tick budget: CraftSession progress evaluator does not spike frame time**
- **GIVEN** a maximum-load scenario: `BENCH_MAX_CRAFTERS = 2` benches fully occupied across a squad, all running simultaneously (maximum 1 active CraftSession per bench, up to N safe rooms' worth of benches in the level)
- **WHEN** `RunService.Heartbeat` fires at 60 Hz
- **THEN** the aggregate time spent by the Crafting Service's per-Heartbeat progress evaluation (the D.1 clock-elapsed closed-form loop across all active CraftSessions — a constant-work recompute per session, round-5 R4-3) is < 0.5 ms per Heartbeat on the server; measured via Roblox MicroProfiler tag `CraftingService_ProgressTick`
- **Test type**: Performance (advisory — REQUIRES PLAYTEST/profiler measurement; round-7: relabeled from "Logic (performance)" — a wall-clock budget is not a deterministic Logic assertion)
- **Source**: `.claude/docs/technical-preferences.md` (60 Hz server, performance budgets)

**H.64 [P2] — RemoteEvent throughput: multi-bench burst stays under global rate**
- **GIVEN** 4 players each send 2 distinct Crafting RemoteEvents within a 1 s window (8 total events across all players)
- **WHEN** the global rate limiter evaluates per-player budgets
- **THEN** each individual player's event count is 2, well below `CRAFTING_GLOBAL_RATE_LIMIT = 8 events/s/player`; no events are dropped; no false positive rate-limit fires
- **Test type**: Logic (the rate-limiter budget check is a deterministic per-player counter assertion — round-7: dropped the "REQUIRES PLAYTEST" qualifier; it is testable without playtest, unlike the wall-clock budget ACs H.63/H.66)
- **Source**: C.15, G.7

**H.65 [P0] — DataStore write count: zero during a RunSession**
- **GIVEN** a full RunSession runs from start to `RunEnded` including Beacon craft + place + activate
- **WHEN** `RunEnded` fires
- **THEN** `DataStoreService` (or ProfileStore) has been called zero times by `CraftingService` for any Crafting state mutation during that run; all Crafting state (`PersonalInventory`, `SquadInventory`, `CraftSession`, `RunSession` flags, `ActiveDeployables`, `EquippedEffects`) exists only in server memory; cleared at `RunEnded`
- **Test type**: Logic (round-7: relabeled from Integration — a `DataStoreService` call-count of zero is a spy/mock assertion on the service surface, testable hermetically without a live DataStore or a second system)
- **Source**: C.4, C.10, F.7

**H.66 [P2] — Signal Anchor sensor check does not exceed budget**
- **GIVEN** a Signal Anchor is live for its full `SIGNAL_ANCHOR_LIFETIME = 45 s`
- **WHEN** the per-Anchor proximity check loop runs at `SENSOR_CHECK_HZ = 5 Hz`
- **THEN** the loop fires at most 225 times (45 × 5) during the Anchor's lifetime; total server CPU cost of the 225 distance-checks is < 0.1 ms cumulative (negligible at the per-check scale of a single distance comparison); no Heartbeat spike attributable to Anchor proximity logic
- **Test type**: Performance (advisory; round-7: relabeled from "Logic (performance)" — the < 0.1 ms wall-clock budget is profiler-measured, not a deterministic Logic assertion; the *count* of 225 checks is deterministic but the budget is the AC's point)
- **Source**: C.7.2, G.5

### H.67–H.72 — Cosmetic Boundary ACs

**H.67 [P2] — Bench cosmetic: no craft-time modification**
- **GIVEN** a cosmetic skin is applied to a bench (changes texture / progress-ring style / recipe icon art)
- **WHEN** a recipe is crafted with the skin applied
- **THEN** `CRAFT_BASE_DWELL_TIME` for each recipe matches the locked values in C.2 (Oxygen Canister 2 s, Dampener Coil 5 s, Signal Anchor 5 s, Escape Beacon 10 s — the 4-recipe round-5 catalog, Squad Relay cut); the cosmetic skin's data contains no override for craft time; verified by a pre-ship cosmetic review checklist gate
- **Test type**: Config-Data (REQUIRES PLAYTEST checklist gate)
- **Source**: C.3.8

**H.68 [P2] — Bench cosmetic: burst-at-completion magnitude not hidden or altered**
- **GIVEN** a cosmetic skin is applied to a bench
- **WHEN** a craft completes (BT3 fires) and `DisturbanceService:Emit` is called
- **THEN** the emission magnitude matches the recipe's locked `MAGNITUDE_CRAFT_*` constant from C.2; the cosmetic contains no visual suppression of the completion-burst feedback (the HUD's burst indicator fires normally); verified by cosmetic review checklist
- **Test type**: Config-Data (REQUIRES PLAYTEST checklist gate)
- **Source**: C.3.8

**H.69 [P2] — Beacon cosmetic: bounding volume unchanged**
- **GIVEN** a cosmetic skin is applied to the Beacon BasePart
- **WHEN** `RequestBeaconPlace` is processed with the cosmetic active
- **THEN** the Beacon BasePart's bounding volume (used for placement overlap check) is identical to the default Beacon BasePart bounding volume; the cosmetic skin does not scale, shift, or replace the BasePart's collision extents; verified by cosmetic review checklist
- **Test type**: Config-Data (REQUIRES PLAYTEST checklist gate)
- **Source**: C.5.8

**H.70 [P2] — Beacon cosmetic: activation emission magnitude unchanged**
- **GIVEN** a cosmetic skin is applied to the Beacon BasePart
- **WHEN** BCT3 fires
- **THEN** `DisturbanceService:Emit("Beacon", beaconWorldPosition, 0.95, beaconPlacerId)` is called with `initialMagnitude = MAGNITUDE_BEACON = 0.95` regardless of which cosmetic skin is active; the cosmetic contains no data field that routes to the magnitude parameter
- **Test type**: Config-Data (REQUIRES PLAYTEST checklist gate)
- **Source**: C.5.8

**H.71 [P2] — Signal Anchor cosmetic: detection radius unchanged**
- **GIVEN** a cosmetic skin is applied to a Signal Anchor
- **WHEN** the predator proximity check runs
- **THEN** the detection threshold used is `SIGNAL_ANCHOR_DETECTION_RADIUS = 30 studs` regardless of which cosmetic is active; the cosmetic cannot set a `detectionRadius` override; verified by cosmetic review checklist (the dual-clause cosmetic-boundary test)
- **Test type**: Config-Data (REQUIRES PLAYTEST checklist gate)
- **Source**: C.7, game-concept cosmetic-boundary dual-clause

**H.72 [P2] — Cosmetic review gate: dual-clause checklist is a hard approval gate**
- **GIVEN** any new cosmetic affecting a bench, Beacon, or Signal Anchor is submitted for review
- **WHEN** the cosmetic review checklist gate runs
- **THEN** the checklist verifies both clauses: (1) no predator-AI input modification (emission position, magnitude, or detection radius unaffected); (2) no player-survival-decision modification (craft time, material cost, recipe visibility unaffected); a cosmetic that fails either clause is REJECTED and cannot ship
- **Test type**: Config-Data (REQUIRES PLAYTEST checklist gate)
- **Source**: C.3.8, C.5.8, C.7, game-concept anti-pillars

### H.73–H.80 — Round-2 Additions (survival window, schema, networking)

Added by the round-2 patch (2026-06-01). H.73–H.78 validate the new survival-window win/lose model; H.79–H.80 validate the schema + networking blocker fixes. The retired ACs (H.18/H.19/H.20/H.34/H.35/H.36/H.59) freed no numbers (numbers are not reused); these new ACs append at H.73+.

**H.73 [P0] — Window-survival victory: ≥requiredHolders alive AND within BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS at window-end fires T8 (B1, holder-threshold round-15)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 (activated); the test sets `_clock` to exactly `RunSession.beaconActivationTime + BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW` (C.16 seam); at the window-end `_step`, at least `requiredHolders = ceil(#aliveMembers/2)` squad members are alive AND within `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12 studs` of `RunSession.beaconWorldPosition` (**the gloss `ceil(#aliveMembers/2)` is the C.10 shorthand for the frozen `ceil(peakAliveCount/2)` baseline clamped per tick by `min(baseline, #aliveMembers)` — round-19 R18-1**; the test runs at squad sizes 2 and 4 to exercise `requiredHolders` = 1 and 2)
- **WHEN** the per-Heartbeat survival-window check evaluates on that `_step`
- **THEN** — split into two separately-gated parts so the sprint evidence requirement is unambiguous:
  - **Part 1 — Sprint-gate (Logic, BLOCKING, testable now via C.16):** BCT4 fires; `RunSession.beaconWindowSurvived = true`; `OnBeaconWindowSurvived(timestamp)` fires **exactly once** to the squad; the Beacon BasePart is removed via the run-end cleanup path; the survival-window + decay-watch timers are cleared; **no T8/`OnBeaconWindowSurvived` fired earlier at activation (H.15)**. This part is fully deterministic and IS the sprint-exit gate for this AC.
  - **Part 2 — Deferred (Integration, ADVISORY, unverified-until-PC):** that `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` actually drives PC's T8 (squad escape → victory) and ends the run in victory. This **cannot pass until Player Controller's T8 subscription is authored** (PC in MAJOR REVISION round-6; F.4/F.6) and is NOT a sprint-exit blocker for the Crafting sprint — it is verified when PC lands.
  - A complementary negative (Part 1 scope): with the same window-end timing but **fewer than `requiredHolders` within `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS`** at the window-end tick (the snapshot below threshold — e.g. a 4-squad with only 1 in radius, `requiredHolders = 2`), BCT4 does NOT fire — the continuous line-break path owns the outcome (H.85/H.114). **Round-13/15 (continuous-hold + threshold):** reaching this victory tick at-or-above threshold means the hold was maintained continuously — a mid-window drop below `requiredHolders` that exhausted `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` would have fired BCT-DEFEAT(scatter) and set `runOutcomeResolved` *before* this tick (so the window handler would have returned at the top-of-`_step` guard); victory is therefore only reachable by an unbroken (or within-grace-recovered) hold.
- **Test type**: Part 1 Logic (sprint-gate, via C.16); Part 2 Integration (**unverified-until-PC** — PC.T8 ← `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` per F.4) — *(round-5: relabeled from [P0] Logic per round-2 B3 / round-4 #9; round-5 qa-gate: split into a blockable Logic part + a deferred Integration part so the sprint gate is unambiguous)*
- **Source**: C.5.6a, C.9 BCT4, C.12.1, C.16; **cross-GDD lock: PC.T8 ← `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` (F.4 forward obligation, unverified-until-PC)**

**H.74 [P0] — Server crash mid-craft: no persistence, no resume, no material loss (`_simulateRestart` seam)**
