- **GIVEN** a `CraftSession` is active (BS2/BS3, `craftProgress` between 0 and 1); the test invokes the C.16 `_simulateRestart()` seam, which discards all in-memory `CraftingService` state and re-initialises (modelling a server process restart — the deterministic, reproducible stand-in for a real crash, per B9)
- **WHEN** `CraftingService` re-initialises after `_simulateRestart()`
- **THEN** no `CraftSession` survives (purely-runtime state, per C.4 / Pillar 4); the bench is in `BS1`; no materials were deducted (deduction is atomic at BT3 only); the player's `PersonalInventory` is intact; there is no resume path and no `DataStoreService` read/write attributable to the lost session
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.4, C.10 (server-crash note), C.9, F.7, C.16

**H.75 [P0] — RequestBeaconPlace / RequestPlaceItem clamp client position to server-tracked range (B13)**
- **GIVEN** a client fires `RequestBeaconPlace(position)` with a `position` that is 40 studs from the player's server-tracked `HumanoidRootPart.Position` (well beyond `BEACON_PLACE_MAX_RANGE = 8 studs`)
- **WHEN** the server validates the request
- **THEN** the raw client `position` is NOT used as the placement coordinate; the server clamps the candidate to within 8 studs of the server-tracked player position and raycasts-to-floor from the clamped point; if no valid floor results, `BeaconRejected(reason="invalid-placement")` fires; in no case is a beacon spawned at the 40-stud client-claimed location. Identical handling for `RequestPlaceItem` (Signal Anchor — round-5: Relay cut).
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.5.5, C.15, E.22

**H.76 [P0] — Full-squad wipe during window: defeat, no victory/decay signal (death OR disconnect)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; the last living squad member departs before `_clock() - beaconActivationTime >= BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW` — either their `Humanoid.Died` **or their `Players.PlayerRemoving`** (clean disconnect — round-5 R4-5) fires
- **WHEN** that departure is drained from `_pendingDepartures` on the BC4 Heartbeat handler (C.8/C.9 buffer)
- **THEN** — split into two separately-gated parts:
  - **Part 1 — Sprint-gate (Logic, BLOCKING, testable now via C.16):** the BC4 drain empties `aliveMembers`, sets `RunSession.runOutcomeResolved = true`, and **fires `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="wipe")` exactly once** (round-9 single decision point, Blocker #5 — NOT routed through PC's all-dead path, which the pre-round-9 wording incorrectly claimed); the Beacon BasePart is removed via the run-end cleanup path; the survival-window + decay-watch timers are cleared; **`OnBeaconWindowSurvived` does NOT fire** and **`OnBeaconDecayed` does NOT fire**. This part is fully deterministic and IS the sprint-exit gate for this AC.
  - **Part 2 — Deferred (Integration, ADVISORY, unverified-until-PC):** that the run actually ends in `RunEnded(defeat)` — PC subscribes to `OnBeaconWindowFailed`, maps `reason="wipe"` to `RunEnded(defeat)`, and **suppresses its own all-dead path during BC4** (round-11 Convergence #2) so there is no double-fire; **unverified until PC is authored** (PC in MAJOR REVISION), and is NOT a sprint-exit blocker for the Crafting sprint.
- **Test type**: Part 1 Logic (sprint-gate, via C.16); Part 2 Integration (**unverified-until-PC** — PC maps `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="wipe")` → `RunEnded(defeat)` + BC4 all-dead suppression, F.4/F.6) — *(round-5: relabeled from [P0] Logic per round-2 B3 / round-4 #9; disconnect coverage added per R4-5; round-5 qa-gate: split into blockable Logic + deferred Integration; round-9 wipe re-routed to `OnBeaconWindowFailed`; round-11 reconciled here)*
- **Source**: C.9 BCT-DEFEAT (wipe, single decision point), E.24, C.12.1, H.89; **cross-GDD lock: PC maps `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="wipe")` → `RunEnded(defeat)` + suppresses all-dead during BC4 (unverified-until-PC)**

**H.77 [P0] — Window tie-break: death OR disconnect of a held member on the exact window-end Heartbeat favors the squad**
- **GIVEN** the test sets `_clock` to exactly `beaconActivationTime + BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW`; at the top of that `_step` the pre-drain `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot **meets `requiredHolders`**; the test injects, on that same `_step`, the departure of a held member such that draining it would drop the in-radius count **below** `requiredHolders` — either their `Humanoid.Died` **or their `Players.PlayerRemoving`** (round-5 R4-5) — buffered into `_pendingDepartures` (C.16 seam makes "same tick" exact)
- **WHEN** the single BC4 Heartbeat handler evaluates exit conditions in declared order (C.9): window-survival check, then `_pendingDepartures` drain
- **THEN** window-survival is evaluated FIRST, reading the per-tick **`windowAliveInRadius` snapshot** (C.10) built at the top of the tick (before the buffered same-tick departure is drained) and comparing it to the pre-drain `requiredHolders`: since the pre-drain snapshot met threshold, BCT4 fires (victory, `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`); both BCT-DEFEAT paths (wipe + scatter) are suppressed because the run is already terminal when the drain runs; the result is **deterministic victory every run** for this input. (Round-7 A-1/A-2: the snapshot is the single source of truth this AC reads — there is no separate live-position read; see H.99.) The determinism comes from the two checks being ordered statements in one handler (R4-5) — NOT from Heartbeat connection priority (which Roblox does not expose) — so it holds in production, not just under the injected `_step`.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.9 tie-break + `_pendingDepartures` buffer, E.25, B1 proximity, C.16

**H.78 [P0] — OnBeaconWindowSurvived fires exactly once per RunSession**
- **GIVEN** BCT4 fires (window survived)
- **WHEN** `OnBeaconWindowSurvived(timestamp)` is fired
- **THEN** the signal fires to the squad channel exactly once; `RunSession.beaconWindowSurvived` is set true and the survival-window timer is cleared on the same tick, so no second elapse can re-fire it; a subsequent Heartbeat does not re-fire (sibling guarantee to H.56 for `OnEscapeBeaconActivated`)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.9 BCT4, C.15

**H.79 [P0] — CraftSession schema carries benchPosition + primaryCrafterId, used by D.3/D.7**
- **GIVEN** a `CraftSession` is created at BT1
- **WHEN** the session record is inspected and a Craft / midpulse emission fires
- **THEN** `CraftSession.benchPosition` is present and equals the bench's immutable world position, and every D.3 / D.7 `Emit` call passes it as `position`; `CraftSession.primaryCrafterId` is present, equals the BT1 initiator's UserId, and is NEVER reassigned across BT2/BT4 — the D.7 midpulse and BCT1 `beaconCrafterId` both read `primaryCrafterId`, not `crafters[1]`
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.10, D.3, D.7

**H.80 [P0] — RemoteEvent handlers do not yield before state mutation — ALL 7 state-mutating handlers (B7/B14; round-5: was 8)**
- **GIVEN** **all seven** state-mutating Crafting server handlers whose race-safety relies on Luau single-threaded sequencing: `RequestCraft` (session create — E.4), `RequestCraftCancel` (session clear), `RequestBeaconPlace` (place + inventory remove — B4/B13), `RequestBeaconActivate` (activate flag + Emit — E.9/E.22), **`RequestUseItem`** (consume from `SquadInventory` — double-consume race), **`RequestEquipItem`** (consume + `EquippedEffects` write — double-equip race, C.6 atomic order), **`RequestPlaceItem`** (Signal Anchor spawn + inventory remove — double-spawn race). *(Round-5 R4-2: `RequestActivateRelay` (the 8th handler) is removed with the Squad Relay cut — the double-transfer race it guarded no longer exists.)*
- **WHEN** each of the seven handlers is statically inspected from entry to its first authoritative state mutation (flag set, inventory deduct/consume, session create, `EquippedEffects` write, deployable spawn)
- **THEN** for **every** handler, no yielding call (`task.wait`, `:WaitForChild`, async `:GetAsync`, event `:Wait`, or any coroutine yield) executes on the path between validation and the state mutation; the validate→mutate sequence is atomic within one Heartbeat, preserving the single-threaded race-guarantee the edge cases depend on. *(Round-3 B7: the round-2 AC covered only 3 of 8 handlers; round-3 extended to all 8; round-5 drops to 7 with the `RequestActivateRelay` removal — UseItem/EquipItem double-consume and PlaceItem double-spawn remain in scope.)*
- **Test type**: Logic (static analysis — the C.16 seam is not needed; this is a source-level lint)
- **Source**: E.4, E.9, E.22, C.6 (equip atomic order), C.9 concurrency guarantees

### H.81–H.87 — Round-3 Additions (proximity victory, placement, determinism, run-config)

Added by the round-3 authoring pass (2026-06-01). H.81 (BT3 tie-break), H.82 (D.1 guard), H.83 (cross-wall LOS), H.84 (squad-size/lone-survivor), H.85 (scatter defeat), H.86 (Heartbeat-pinned timer), H.87 (tap-hold). Numbers append at H.81+; none reuse a retired number.

**H.81 [P0] — BT3 completion wins a same-Heartbeat tie vs. cancel/death/disconnect/range-violation (buffered)**
- **GIVEN** a sole crafter on a recipe whose `craftProgress` will cross `1.0` on a specific `_step`; the test injects, on that same `_step` (C.16 seam), a `RequestCraftCancel` (or the crafter's `Humanoid.Died`, **or their `Players.PlayerRemoving` disconnect** — round-5 R4-5, or an out-of-range condition for the watcher) into `_pendingDepartures`
- **WHEN** the server processes that Heartbeat with the C.8 ordering: **D.1 closed-form progress + completion check FIRST, then `_pendingDepartures` drain SECOND**
- **THEN** BT3 fires: materials deducted, item granted, `DisturbanceService:Emit` fired for the crafter, `OnCraftCompleted` fired, session cleared; the same-tick buffered cancel/death/disconnect/range-violation then drains to find no active session and is a no-op (no BT5, no double-processing). The result is **deterministic completion every run** for this input — not dependent on Roblox callback-ordering luck, because both checks live in one Heartbeat handler (R4-5). (The one carve-out: if the atomic BT3 deduction itself fails for insufficient materials, BT6 fires — completion-failure, not the tie-break.)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.8 tie-break + `_pendingDepartures` buffer, E.29, D.1, C.16

**H.82 [P0] — D.1 startup guard rejects non-positive CRAFT_BASE_DWELL_TIME**
- **GIVEN** a `CraftingService` config in which some recipe's `CRAFT_BASE_DWELL_TIME` is `0` (or any value `< CRAFT_DWELL_TIME_FLOOR = 0.5 s`)
- **WHEN** `CraftingService` runs its startup config-validation
- **THEN** the service raises a hard startup error (refuses to start) identifying the offending recipe; it does NOT start with the degenerate value (which would make D.1's `1 / CRAFT_BASE_DWELL_TIME` divide by zero → `craftRate = inf` → instant craft). A valid config (all recipes `>= 0.5 s`) passes validation and the service starts normally.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: D.1 domain guard, G.3 floor

**H.83 [P0] — Cross-wall placement rejected by LOS reachability raycast (B4)**
- **GIVEN** a player fires `RequestBeaconPlace(position)` (or `RequestPlaceItem`) targeting a point within `BEACON_PLACE_MAX_RANGE = 8 studs` that has a valid floor beneath it but lies on the far side of a wall from the player's server-tracked position
- **WHEN** the server runs the C.5.5 validation order (clamp → LOS raycast → floor → overlap)
- **THEN** the LOS reachability raycast (player → clamped candidate) detects the obstructing wall and the placement is rejected with `BeaconRejected(reason="unreachable-placement")`; no BasePart spawns; the item is not consumed; `RunSession.beaconPlaced` (or `ActiveDeployables`) is unchanged. A clear-line placement at the same distance succeeds.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.5.5, C.15, E.28

**H.84 — Squad-size assumption enforced upstream; lone survivor can still craft**
- **GIVEN** (a) the run-start assumption: `CraftingService` init asserts `squadSize >= MIN_SQUAD_SIZE = 2`; and (b) a mid-run state where a run that started with squadSize ≥ 2 has dropped to exactly one alive member who holds materials in their own `PersonalInventory`
- **WHEN** (a) `CraftingService` initialises with `squadSize = 1` (simulating a misconfigured/unsupported solo start), and separately (b) the lone survivor fires `RequestCraft` for a recipe their own materials afford
- **THEN** (a) the init assertion fails fast (solo start is unsupported, C.17 — the upstream gate should have prevented it; the assertion is defence-in-depth); and (b) the lone survivor's craft proceeds normally drawing from their own (never-locked) `PersonalInventory`, and any already-crafted item in the squad shared pool remains usable — no soft-lock from the dead-player inventory lock
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.17, C.3.6, C.4, E.30

**H.85 [P0] — Scatter / line-break defeat: hold below `requiredHolders` for the full grace fires `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="scatter")` (B1 round-13 fail-fast, holder-threshold round-15)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 with a 4-member squad (`requiredHolders = 2` — the frozen `ceil(peakAliveCount/2)` baseline at a full 4-member activation, C.10 shorthand, round-19 R18-1); via the C.16 `_clock`/`_step` + `_setMemberPosition` seams, all members are alive but **only 1** is within `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12 studs` (the `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot is **below threshold**, `1 < 2` — exercising SENTRY: one sentry cannot hold for four), and the test holds the count below threshold for a **continuous `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`** (advancing `_clock` past `_lineBreakSince + BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`). **This may occur at ANY point during the window, not only at window-end** (round-13 continuous-hold)
- **WHEN** the BC4 handler computes the line-break in step (b) and resolves it in step (c) on the tick the grace elapses (round-15 B-1 — compute-in-(b), fire-in-(c) after the drains, gated on `aliveMembers` non-empty)
- **THEN** BCT-DEFEAT fires on the scatter / line-break path: because members are alive (`aliveMembers` non-empty after the drains), **Crafting fires `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="scatter")` exactly once** (PC maps it to `RunEnded(defeat)` — PC's all-dead path is NOT what ends this run); `runOutcomeResolved` is set; Beacon BasePart removed via run-end cleanup; timers cleared; **`OnBeaconWindowSurvived` does NOT fire and `OnBeaconDecayed` does NOT fire**. **Complementary positive (round-13/15):** if enough alive members re-enter the radius (back to `>= requiredHolders`) *before* the grace elapses, NO defeat fires and `_lineBreakSince` resets to `nil` (H.114). (Boundary: a member at exactly `12.0` studs counts as within radius — the test is `<=` — H.92.)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.5.6a, C.9 BCT-DEFEAT step b, E.27, C.10 `_lineBreakSince`, C.16

**H.86 [P0] — Survival-window timer is Heartbeat-polled (not task.delay) AND departures are buffered, not direct (B5 + R4-5, static)**
- **GIVEN** the `CraftingService` code path that starts and evaluates the Beacon survival window at/after BCT3, plus the `Humanoid.Died` and `Players.PlayerRemoving` handlers active during BC4
- **WHEN** the paths are statically inspected
- **THEN** (a) window completion is detected by a per-`RunService.Heartbeat` (C.16 `_step`) comparison `_clock() - RunSession.beaconActivationTime >= BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW`; there is **no** `task.delay(BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW, …)` (or `task.wait`-based) callback driving window-end; **AND (b, round-5 R4-5)** the `Humanoid.Died` / `Players.PlayerRemoving` callbacks during BC4 do **not** mutate run/alive/window state directly — they only append to `_pendingDepartures`, which is drained **inside** the BC4 Heartbeat handler **after** the window-survival check. Together these are the static guarantee that the E.25/H.77 same-Heartbeat tie-break is deterministic in production: window-elapse and departure resolution are two ordered statements in one handler, not two callbacks racing on a connection-priority API Roblox does not provide.
- **Test type**: Logic (static analysis)
- **Source**: C.5.6, C.8/C.9 buffer + tie-break, B5, R4-5

**H.87 [P2] — Beacon placement and activation require a tap-hold commit (B6, UI)**
- **GIVEN** the client-side beacon placement and activation flows
- **WHEN** a player initiates a beacon place or activate
- **THEN** the client emits `RequestBeaconPlace` / `RequestBeaconActivate` only after a sustained tap-hold of `TAP_HOLD_COMMIT_DURATION = 0.5 s` (a tap-hold radial on touch per art-bible §7.7; a held key/button on PC/gamepad); a single quick tap does NOT fire the request. Bench-open and Signal Anchor placement remain single-tap (round-5: relay-activate removed); recipe-commit remains two-tap (UI.2). This is the friction model against an accidental, irreversible placement/activation.
- **Test type**: UI (manual walkthrough or interaction test — advisory)
- **Source**: C.5.2, C.12.4, UI.2, B6

### H.88–H.92 — Round-5 Additions (AC coverage gaps)

Added by the round-5 authoring pass (2026-06-02) to close the round-4 AC-coverage gaps. H.88 (double-equip behavioral outcome), H.89 (window survived/failed mutual-exclusion), H.90 (BindToClose-during-BC4), H.91 (beaconActivationTime invariant), H.92 (exact-12.0-stud hold boundary — round-15 SENTRY radius; round-19 stale-label fix, was "30.0-stud"). Numbers append at H.88+; none reuse a retired number. *(The round-4 list also requested a `RequestActivateRelay` rate-limit AC — moot, the Relay was cut R4-2.)*

**H.88 [P0] — RequestEquipItem double-equip: only one Coil consumed, second rejected (behavioral, C.6 atomic order)**
- **GIVEN** a player with no active Dampener Coil and exactly one Coil in `SquadInventory` (`count = 1`); the test invokes the server-side `RequestEquipItem` handler function **twice in direct sequence, with no yield and no Heartbeat advance between the two calls** (both carrying `{itemId="DampenerCoil"}` for the same `playerId`). *(This models two `RequestEquipItem` RemoteEvents arriving on the same Heartbeat — Luau single-threaded sequencing, NOT the `_pendingDepartures` buffer; the C.16 `_step` seam is not the injection mechanism here, it is the handler itself called back-to-back.)*
- **WHEN** the server processes both calls through the C.6 atomic equip handler (validate no-active-Coil guard → decrement → write `EquippedEffects[playerId].dampenerCoil` → fire `OnEquippedEffectStarted`, no yield between steps per H.80)
- **THEN** Luau single-threaded sequencing applies: the first request equips the Coil (`SquadInventory.DampenerCoil.count = 0`, one `EquippedEffects[playerId].dampenerCoil` instance, one `OnEquippedEffectStarted`); the second sees the active instance from step 3's write and is rejected (`OnCraftRejected(reason="already-equipped")`), consuming **no** second item. The Coil cannot be double-consumed. (This is the behavioral complement to H.80's static no-yield check.)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.6 (equip atomic order), C.15 `RequestEquipItem`, H.80

**H.89 [P0] — Window-end fires exactly one of OnBeaconWindowSurvived / OnBeaconWindowFailed (mutual exclusion)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; the test runs three variants via the C.16 seam — (a) the window elapses with `#windowAliveInRadius >= requiredHolders` (held unbroken); (b) members alive but `#windowAliveInRadius < requiredHolders` for the full `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` (continuous line-break scatter — round-13/15, can resolve mid-window, not only at window-end); (c) all members dead/gone (wipe — `aliveMembers` emptied, immediate, precedence over scatter — B-1)
- **WHEN** the BC4 Heartbeat handler resolves the terminal outcome in each variant
- **THEN** **exactly one** terminal window signal is emitted: (a) `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` only (NOT `OnBeaconWindowFailed`); (b) `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="scatter")` only (NOT `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`); (c) `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="wipe")` only (NOT `OnBeaconWindowSurvived`) — **round-9's single decision point routes the BC4 wipe through Crafting's own signal, not PC's all-dead path** (Blocker #5; the pre-round-9 "neither signal fires, wipe via PC's all-dead path" reading was the round-10 reconciliation miss this AC corrects, alongside E.24 / H.76 / C.12.1). `OnBeaconDecayed` is suppressed in all three. In no variant do both `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` and `OnBeaconWindowFailed` fire, and in no variant does either fire more than once.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.9 BCT4 vs BCT-DEFEAT mutual-exclusion (single decision point — all three outcomes), C.12.1, E.24, E.27, C.16

**H.90 [P0] — BindToClose during BC4: forced cleanup, no victory/defeat/decay signal**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 (activated; window counting down); the test invokes the C.16 **`_simulateBindToClose()`** seam (round-7 D-1 — the deterministic, headless stand-in for `game:BindToClose`: Studio stop / server migration / scheduled shutdown), so the AC is testable in TestEZ/Lemur without triggering a real server shutdown
- **WHEN** the `_simulateBindToClose()` handler path runs
- **THEN** the run terminates via the E.8 forced-cleanup path: the Beacon BasePart is torn down; **`OnBeaconWindowSurvived` does NOT fire, `OnBeaconWindowFailed` does NOT fire, and `OnBeaconDecayed` does NOT fire**; no victory/defeat result is awarded (no in-run save, Pillar 4); the handler returns promptly without blocking on the survival-window timer (it does not `task.wait` the remaining window). RunSession-scoped state is cleared.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.9 BindToClose-during-BC4, E.31, E.8

**H.91 [P0] — beaconActivationTime != nil iff beaconActivated invariant**
- **GIVEN** a RunSession traversed through BC1→BC2→BC3→BC4 (the test drives BCT1/BCT2/BCT3 via the seam)
- **WHEN** `RunSession.beaconActivationTime` and `RunSession.beaconActivated` are inspected at each state
- **THEN** the invariant `beaconActivationTime != nil ⟺ beaconActivated == true` holds at every observed state: both are `nil`/`false` before BCT3, and both become set together at BCT3 step 1+2 (never one without the other); no code path sets `beaconActivationTime` while `beaconActivated == false`, nor reads `beaconActivationTime` without first guarding on `beaconActivated`. (Detects any BCT3-ordering or rollback bug.)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.10 RunSession invariants, C.9 BCT3

**H.92 [P0] — Exact 12.0-stud hold boundary counts as in-radius (`<=`, secures victory)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 with a **lone** alive member (`requiredHolders = ceil(1/2) = 1`); the test sets `_clock` to exactly window-end and positions that member at `dist3D(member, beaconWorldPosition) == 12.0` studs exactly (`== BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS`)
- **WHEN** the BCT4 proximity clause evaluates `dist3D(...) <= BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` and compares the in-radius count (1) to `requiredHolders` (1)
- **THEN** the member at exactly 12.0 counts as **within** radius (the test is `<=`, not `<`), so `#windowAliveInRadius = 1 >= requiredHolders = 1`: BCT4 fires → `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` → victory. A complementary negative with the sole member positioned at **`13.0 studs`** from `beaconWorldPosition` (unambiguously outside `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12.0` — a full stud clear of the boundary, no float-rounding ambiguity) starts the line-break grace on that tick and yields scatter-defeat (`OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="scatter")`) **only after `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` elapses with the member still outside** (NOT an immediate terminal on the window-end tick — the grace must run first; H.85). This pins the boundary contract so an implementer's `<` vs `<=` choice is not left to interpretation.
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.5.6a, C.9 BCT4 proximity clause, H.85, C.16

### H.93–H.101 — Round-7 Additions (Group-B canister counter, Group-A win-condition data model, Group-C signal, recommended-tail coverage)

Added by the round-7 authoring pass (2026-06-02). H.93 (in-window canister emission), H.94 (hold-state signal), H.95 (WCAG flash cap on Crafting surfaces), H.96 (BT2 recipe-mismatch rejection), H.97 (BT4 rate-anchor reset), H.98 (global-limit burst window), H.99 (alive-set single source of truth), H.100 (`benchMaxCraftersEffective` storage), H.101 (dual-scope departure dispatch). Numbers append at H.93+; none reuse a retired number.

**H.93 [P0] — In-window Oxygen Canister use emits at the beacon position; outside BC4 it is silent (Group-B bunker counter)**
- **GIVEN** an alive player holds an Oxygen Canister in `SquadInventory`; three variants via the C.16 seam — (a) the survival window is genuinely live (`beaconActivated == true && not runOutcomeResolved`); (b) the beacon is NOT yet active (pre-activation, BC2/BC3); **(c) the beacon WAS active but the run has terminally resolved — specifically a *defeat* (scatter or wipe), so `beaconWindowSurvived == false` but `runOutcomeResolved == true` (the round-16 network BLOCKING-1 case)**
- **WHEN** the player fires `RequestUseItem("OxygenCanister")`
- **THEN** in variant (a): after the RM restore, **exactly one** `DisturbanceService:Emit("Craft", RunSession.beaconWorldPosition, MAGNITUDE_CANISTER_INWINDOW_USE = 0.18, usingPlayerId)` fires — `position` is the beacon's server-tracked world position (round-11 EC-3 — NOT the player position, NOT `benchPosition`, NOT a client-supplied value: the `RequestUseItem` payload has no position field), `sourcePlayerId` is the using player; **a complementary check positions the using player well outside `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` and asserts the emission still fires at `beaconWorldPosition`, not at the player** (the scatter-to-breathe foreclosure); in variant (b): **zero** `DisturbanceService:Emit` calls fire (silent — the disturbance was paid at craft); **in variant (c): zero `DisturbanceService:Emit` calls fire (round-16 — the `not runOutcomeResolved` guard makes a post-defeat use silent; a guard of `beaconWindowSurvived == false` would WRONGLY emit here, into an ended run at a stale `beaconWorldPosition`).** In all variants the Canister is consumed and `OnOxygenPulseRequest` fires to RM. No beacon completion/attribution flag (`beaconActivated`, `beaconWindowSurvived`, `beaconPlacerId`) is read or written by this path (it reads `beaconWorldPosition` only as the emission position, and `runOutcomeResolved` only as the live-window guard).
- **Test type**: Logic (BC4 state driven via the C.16 seam)
- **Source**: C.6, C.11, C.15, G.4

**H.94 [P0] — OnBeaconHoldStateChanged fires on BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS boundary-cross during BC4; presentation-only**
- **GIVEN** the beacon is in BC4; a squad member starts outside `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` of `RunSession.beaconWorldPosition`, moves inside, then moves back outside
- **WHEN** the member crosses the radius boundary in each direction during the window
- **THEN** `OnBeaconHoldStateChanged(playerId, inRadius)` fires once per crossing (`inRadius = true` on entering at `dist <= BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS`, `false` on leaving only at `dist > BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS + BEACON_HOLD_HYSTERESIS` — the round-11 Schmitt band, C.10), detected as the change in the presented set vs the stored `RunSession._windowPrevInRadius`; AND the victory/line-break outcome is **identical** whether or not any intermediate `OnBeaconHoldStateChanged` fired (round-13: the win condition reads the **strict per-tick `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot + the `_lineBreakSince` grace** on every BC4 tick, C.9/C.10 — the hysteresis-banded per-member signal is presentation-only and never feeds the win evaluation, and never reads/writes `_windowPrevInRadius` or `_lineBreakSince`; the win-relevant squad-level cue is the separate strict `OnBeaconLineBreak`, C.14)
- **THEN (negatives — round-11)**: (a) **stays-in-radius fires zero** — a member who stays inside the radius for K consecutive ticks (no boundary cross) fires `OnBeaconHoldStateChanged` exactly zero times after their initial entry (the no-flood guarantee); (b) **boundary jitter fires zero** — a member oscillating within the hysteresis band `(R, R + HYSTERESIS]` (modelling network jitter at the boundary) fires zero leave/enter events once shown in-radius — the round-10 ~120-events/s flood does not occur
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_setMemberPosition` position-injection seam + `_clock`/`_step` — round-9 added the seam the round-8 panel found missing)
- **Source**: C.14, C.15, UI.1 surface 13, C.10 `_windowPrevInRadius`, G.6 `BEACON_HOLD_HYSTERESIS`

**H.95 [P1] — Crafting-owned BC4 activation surfaces flash ≤ 3/second (WCAG 2.3.1), INCLUDING the HOLDING/BROKEN banner (round-15 quadruple-lens; round-19 P2→P1 — it guards the safety-critical win banner, qa nice-to-have)**
- **GIVEN** the beacon is in BC4 and the Crafting-owned activation surfaces render: the survival-window countdown-ring pulse, the `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12` world-ring pulse (Moment 26), the Coil final-10s warning pulse, **and the squad-level HOLDING/BROKEN `OnBeaconLineBreak` banner (surface 13)**
- **WHEN** each surface's animation timing is inspected (animation-config assertion or visual review); for the HOLDING/BROKEN banner specifically, the test drives a **sole boundary-holder oscillating across the 12-stud threshold every tick** (the round-14 N14-B2 flap scenario) via the C.16 `_setMemberPosition`/`_step` seams
- **THEN** none flashes faster than **3 flashes/second** (WCAG 2.3.1). For the banner: because the `broken=false` (restore) edge is gated behind `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE` consecutive at-threshold ticks (C.9 step d / C.10 `_lineBreakRestoreTicks`) while `broken=true` fires immediately, a boundary-oscillating holder produces **at most one HOLDING↔BROKEN transition per debounce window**, never the up-to-60 Hz flap the un-debounced edge would produce — so the banner cannot violate 2.3.1, and the colorblind shape/icon redundancy (ux F3) is present regardless of color. (The beacon-activation *flora* flash cap is a separate, ED-owned obligation per F.4 — this AC covers only the surfaces Crafting renders.)
- **Test type**: UI / Config-Data (advisory — animation-config assertion or review-checklist gate)
- **Source**: UI.5 item 6, F.4 ED row, WCAG 2.3.1

**H.96 [P0] — BT2 join rejects a different recipe than the active session**
- **GIVEN** a bench is in BS2 crafting recipe X with `craftProgress < CRAFT_JOIN_CUTOFF = 0.85`, and `benchMaxCraftersEffective >= 2`; a second player within range fires `RequestCraft(recipeId = Y)` where Y ≠ X
- **WHEN** the server validates the join
- **THEN** the join is rejected with `OnCraftRejected(reason="recipe-mismatch")`; the second player is NOT added to `CraftSession.crafters`; the session continues on recipe X unchanged; the bench stays in BS2 (a joiner must select the SAME recipe to join, per C.3.5 / BT2)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.5, C.8 BT2

**H.97 — D.2/BT4 rate-anchor reset preserves progress at the new N=1 rate (clock-boundary)**
- **GIVEN** a bench in BS3 (N=2) on a 10 s Beacon, `rateAnchorClock = startClock`; the test sets `_clock() = startClock + 2.5` and `_step` to reach `craftProgress == (2.5 × 2)/10.0 == 0.50` **exactly**; then one crafter departs (BT4)
- **WHEN** BT4 fires (crafter removed; N → 1)
- **THEN** the handler captures `CraftSession.progressAtRateAnchor = 0.50` (the current `craftProgress`) and `CraftSession.rateAnchorClock = _clock()` at the departure, and sets `lastCrafterCount = 1` — progress is **preserved**, not discarded; a subsequent `_clock() = departureClock + 5.0` then `_step` yields `craftProgress == 0.50 + (5.0 × 1)/10.0 == 1.0` **exactly** → BT3. All boundary values (0.50, 1.0) are exactly representable — bit-exact, no tolerance
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: D.2 rate-change anchor reset, C.10, C.8 BT4

**H.98 — Global rate-limit burst window: 16 events / 3 s pass, 17th in-window dropped**
- **GIVEN** a player sends 16 distinct Crafting RemoteEvents within a 3 s window (within the round-7-aligned burst allowance of 16 events / 3 s)
- **WHEN** the 16 events and then a 17th event within the same 3 s window are processed
- **THEN** the first 16 pass (the burst window absorbs a legitimate retry burst that the per-event 3 s burst windows permit); the 17th within the same 3 s window is dropped by `CRAFTING_GLOBAL_RATE_LIMIT`; the global burst window (3 s) is aligned with the per-event 3 s burst windows so no legitimate retry burst is falsely tripped
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.15, G.7

**H.99 [P0] — Window-end reads ONE per-tick alive-in-radius snapshot (single source of truth)**
- **GIVEN** the beacon is in BC4 at the window-end `_step` (C.16 seam); a mix of members — some alive-and-in-radius, some alive-but-out-of-radius, and at least one departing on this exact tick via `_enqueueDeparture` (buffered)
- **WHEN** the single BC4 Heartbeat handler builds `windowAliveInRadius` at the top of the tick (before the drain) and evaluates the BCT4 victory check and the BCT-DEFEAT scatter check
- **THEN** both checks read the **same** per-tick `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot — there is no second, independent live `HumanoidRootPart.Position` read; a member buffered for departure on this tick is excluded from the snapshot ONLY via the buffered-departure path (not via a racing live read of a possibly-half-destroyed character); BCT4 and BCT-DEFEAT-scatter are mutually exclusive and exhaustive over that one snapshot (the round-6 "two sources of truth" finding is closed)
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 + `_enqueueDeparture` seam)
- **Source**: C.9 tie-break, C.10 windowAliveInRadius model, H.77

**H.100 [P0] — benchMaxCraftersEffective stored at run start, drives BT2 + D.2, never shrinks**
- **GIVEN** two run-start variants via the seam — (a) `squadSize = 3` and (b) `squadSize = 2`
- **WHEN** the field is inspected at `RunStarted`, BT2 evaluates its entry guard, D.2 reads the cap, and (variant a) a member dies mid-run dropping the alive count to 2
- **THEN** `RunSession.benchMaxCraftersEffective` is stored at run start = `min(BENCH_MAX_CRAFTERS, ceil(squadSize/2))` — **2** for variant (a), **1** for variant (b); BT2's guard reads the stored value (variant b → cap 1 → BS3 unreachable, a second joiner rejected `bench-full`; variant a → cap 2 → join allowed); D.2's `crafterSpeedMultiplier` reads the same stored value; and the field is **NOT recomputed** when the member dies in variant (a) — it remains 2 (the cap never shrinks mid-run, C.3.5)
- **Test type**: Logic
- **Source**: C.3.5, C.8 BT2, D.2, C.10, C.17

**H.101 [P0] — A single departure event updates BOTH the bench and the RunSession BC4 scope (dual-scope dispatch)**
- **GIVEN** a player who is simultaneously (a) registered in an active `CraftSession.crafters` at a bench AND (b) a member of the BC4 window alive-set; the beacon is in BC4
- **WHEN** that player's `Humanoid.Died` (or `Players.PlayerRemoving`) fires, calling `_enqueueDeparture(playerId, reason)` once
- **THEN** the one event fans out to **both** scopes: the bench drain removes the player from `CraftSession.crafters` (firing BT4 if others remain, or BT5 if they were the sole crafter) **AND** the RunSession BC4 drain removes them from `RunSession.aliveMembers` (contributing to the wipe/scatter evaluation); **neither drain consumes the event in a way that hides it from the other** (no single shared consumer queue) — both effects are observed from the one departure. This verifies the `_enqueueDeparture` fan-out (C.8 A-3), closing the round-6 "intent-without-mechanism" finding
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 + `_enqueueDeparture` seam)
- **Source**: C.8 buffer-scope dispatch, C.9, C.10

### H.102–H.107 — Round-9 Additions (schema-first reconciliation: clock guard, alive-set, cancel de-dup, window-range assertion, inverse-degenerate fence, tap-hold release)

Added by the round-9 authoring pass (2026-06-03). H.102 (monotonic-clock / backward-slew guard), H.103 (alive-set is the sole alive-predicate), H.104 (RequestCraftCancel double-fire de-dup), H.105 (window-range fail-fast init assertion), H.106 (RM oxygen-drain floor — closes BOTH the over-supplied bunker line AND the inverse-degenerate tank-the-damage line; forward obligation — round-22 economy EC-22-B1 corrected the prior "inverse-degenerate"-only index label), H.107 (tap-hold release-to-cancel). Numbers append at H.102+; none reuse a retired number.

**H.102 [P0] — Backward server-time slew freezes but never reverses progress; the window still completes on recovery**
- **GIVEN** a `CraftSession` mid-craft and a beacon in BC4, both driven by the C.16 `_clock()` seam; the test advances `_clock()` to an intermediate value (e.g. `rateAnchorClock + 5.0` on a 10 s craft → `craftProgress == 0.5`), then sets `_clock()` to a value **below** the previous reading (modelling an NTP correction / VM-migration backward step)
- **WHEN** the next `_step` recomputes `craftProgress` (D.1) and the BC4 handler re-evaluates the survival-window comparison (C.9)
- **THEN** `craftProgress` does **not** decrease and is never negative/`NaN` (the `max(0.0, _clock() − rateAnchorClock)` term clamps the backward delta to zero elapsed); the survival window does not regress past `BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW`; AND once `_clock()` is advanced forward again past the prior peak, both progress and the window resume from where they froze and completion (BT3 / BCT4) fires normally — a slew can delay but never deny a legitimately-earned completion
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_clock` backward-step injection)
- **Source**: D.1 backward-time-slew guard, C.9, C.16 monotonic-clock contract

**H.103 [P0] — `aliveMembers` is the sole alive-predicate; window eval never reads a live Humanoid**
- **GIVEN** the beacon is in BC4; via the seam, a member is removed from `RunSession.aliveMembers` through a buffered `_enqueueDeparture` drain on a given tick (modelling death/disconnect), while that member's Character/`HumanoidRootPart` is left in a destroyed-or-half-destroyed state
- **WHEN** the BC4 handler builds `windowAliveInRadius` for that tick and any subsequent tick
- **THEN** the departed member is absent from `windowAliveInRadius` **because** it is no longer in `aliveMembers` (not because of a live `Humanoid.Health`/character read); no code path reads a `Humanoid` or `HumanoidRootPart` for a member outside `aliveMembers`; re-adding the member to `aliveMembers` (respawn) is the only way it re-enters the snapshot. Two independent implementations of the BC4 handler driven by the same seam inputs produce identical `windowAliveInRadius` sets (the predicate is unambiguous)
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 + `_enqueueDeparture` + `_setMemberPosition` seams)
- **Source**: C.10 aliveMembers, C.9 window-participant evaluation

**H.104 [P0] — Two `RequestCraftCancel` in one Heartbeat drain the crafter exactly once (de-dup)**
- **GIVEN** a bench in BS2/BS3 with a registered crafter; the test fires `RequestCraftCancel` for that crafter **twice within the same Heartbeat** (both pass the in-handler validation because the caller is still in `crafters` at validation time, before the drain)
- **WHEN** `_enqueueDeparture` is invoked for both, then the bench drain runs in the same `_step`
- **THEN** only **one** entry for that `{playerId, CraftSession-scope}` is queued (the second append is a no-op); the drain removes the crafter exactly once and fires exactly one BT4 (others remain) or BT5 (sole crafter); there is no double-remove and no operation on an already-cleared session. The same de-dup holds independently for the RunSession/window scope
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 + `_enqueueDeparture` seam)
- **Source**: C.8 dispatch de-dup, C.10 `_pendingDepartures`

**H.105 [P0] — CraftingService refuses to start if the derived survival window is outside [35.0, 70.1]s**
- **GIVEN** `CraftingService` init with a resolved `BEACON_HALF_LIFE` (read from the registry / ED); two variants — (a) `90 s` (in-band: derived window ≈ `49.3 s`); (b) `45 s` (out-of-band: derived window ≈ `24.6 s`)
- **WHEN** the service runs its startup config-validation
- **THEN** variant (a) starts normally; variant (b) raises a **hard startup error** (the service refuses to start) because the derived `BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW = BEACON_HALF_LIFE × log₂(MAGNITUDE_BEACON / HUNT_THRESHOLD)` (= `BEACON_HALF_LIFE × log₂(0.95 / 0.65)`, **the same closed form the service computes — NOT the rounded `0.5476` literal**, so **both** mandated-band boundary cases are unambiguous: `64 × log₂(0.95/0.65) = 35.05 s` (lower edge, in-band) and `128 × log₂(0.95/0.65) = 70.08 s` (upper edge, in-band — it exceeds a naive `70.0` ceiling by 0.08 s, which is why the predicate ceiling is `70.1`, matching G.6 L1063/L1065)) falls outside the enforced predicate band `[35.0, 70.1] s` — a degenerate window can never reach a live run regardless of whether ED has applied its registry tightening. The boundary predicate is `derivedWindow >= 35.0 AND derivedWindow <= 70.1` — the `70.1` ceiling admits the **mandated** upper-band edge `BEACON_HALF_LIFE = 128 s → 70.08 s`; a naive `70.0` would wrongly reject a CD-mandated legal config and crash the service at startup (round-22 systems B1). Symmetric rejection for a genuinely too-high half-life (e.g. `180 s` → `~98.6 s`)
- **Test type**: Logic (deterministic config-validation assertion against the closed-form window; the prior dual "Logic / Config-Data" label is settled to **Logic** — round-21 qa B-2)
- **Source**: G.6 Crafting-side init assertion, D.1 fail-fast pattern, F.4 ED row

**H.106 — RM oxygen-drain floor closes both the over-supply and the tank-the-damage lines (forward obligation)**
- **GIVEN** the Resource Management GDD is authored and defines `OXYGEN_DRAIN_BC4_FLOOR` + the finalized `canister_restore`
- **WHEN** the RM AC suite runs at default tunings for a `MIN_SQUAD_SIZE` clustered stationary squad inside `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` over a full `BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW`
- **THEN** (i) a full `ITEM_STACK_MAX` Canister stack used optimally **cannot** keep every member above critical for the whole window (`ITEM_STACK_MAX × canister_restore < BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW × OXYGEN_DRAIN_BC4_FLOOR` per member) — **evaluated at the shortest in-band window (35 s), the worst case for the floor** (round-11 EC-1: at `canister_restore ≈ 3 s` a full stack ≈ 30 s < 35 s; if the inequality holds at 35 s it holds across `[35,70] s`); AND (ii) a member who uses **zero** Canisters in BC4 (the tank-the-damage inverse-degenerate) reaches the critical threshold before window-end — neither over-supply nor abstention is a free silent line
- **Test type**: Integration — **unverified-until-RM** (forward obligation; cannot be executed until the RM GDD + values exist)
- **Source**: F.4 RM row, G.4 `MAGNITUDE_CANISTER_INWINDOW_USE`, C.5.6a aid-necessity fence

**H.107 — Tap-hold release before commit fires no RemoteEvent and shows a platform-specific abort cue**
- **GIVEN** a player begins the beacon place or activate tap-hold (`TAP_HOLD_COMMIT_DURATION = 0.5 s`) on touch, PC, and gamepad
- **WHEN** the player releases **before** 0.5 s
- **THEN** no `RequestBeaconPlace` / `RequestBeaconActivate` RemoteEvent is sent (server-side indistinguishable from no input — verified by asserting zero inbound events at the server); AND the client shows the abort cue per platform (touch: radial arc unwinds to empty; PC: filling ring drains + label reverts; gamepad: ring drains + a single ≤80 ms low-intensity rumble distinct from the success pulse); a release **at or after** 0.5 s commits and fires exactly one event
- **Test type**: UI (advisory — client-side interaction test + server zero-event assertion)
- **Source**: UI.2 release-to-cancel, C.15

### H.108–H.113 — Round-11 Additions (whole-run alive-set, terminal-once, seam integrity, reduced-motion, reason-gated drain, RunEnded latch)

Added by the round-11 authoring pass (2026-06-04) to close the round-10 convergences + single-lens AC-coverage gaps (H.108–H.111) and the round-11 build-from-artifact-gate findings (H.112–H.113). H.108 (whole-run `aliveMembers` maintenance + respawn, split Logic/Integration), H.109 (terminal outcome runs exactly once), H.110 (`_enqueueDeparture` seam-integrity, re-add exempt), H.111 (reduced-motion variants required), H.112 (reason-gated alive-scope drain — build-gate C3), H.113 (`RunEnded` subscription latches + halts the whole-run drain — build-gate C2). Numbers append at H.108+; none reuse a retired number.

**H.108 [P0] — A pre-activation death drains `aliveMembers`, so the first BC4 snapshot never counts a dead player; respawn re-adds (Convergence #1)**
- **GIVEN** a run with `squadSize = 3`; via the C.16 seam a member dies (`_enqueueDeparture`) **before** the beacon reaches BC4, and in a second variant that same member **respawns** before activation — the respawn is driven **through the `_enqueueReadd(playerId)` seam directly** (qa F-T2-1 round-15: the test MUST NOT rely on a real `Player.CharacterAdded`, which is an illegal live dependency in a headless unit test; `_enqueueReadd` is the production re-add entry point, so driving it IS driving production behavior — H.110); a third variant calls `_enqueueReadd(playerId)` **twice in one tick** to exercise de-dup
- **WHEN** the beacon then activates (BCT3) and the first BC4 Heartbeat builds `windowAliveInRadius`
- **THEN** — split into two separately-gated parts (build-gate M2, mirroring H.73/H.76):
  - **Part 1 — Sprint-gate (Logic, BLOCKING, testable now via C.16):** variant 1 — the pre-activation departed member is **absent** from `RunSession.aliveMembers` at BCT3 (the whole-run RunSession-scope drain runs every tick, C.8/C.10, not only during BC4), so the first `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot cannot count the dead player (the round-10 TRIPLE-lens defect); variant 2 — the **buffered re-add** (`_enqueueReadd` → drained in `_step` after the departure drain — round-13 buffered re-add, C.10) re-adds them to `aliveMembers`, after which they are eligible for the snapshot again; **a same-tick death+respawn nets to alive** (the departure drains first, then the re-add). **Variant 3 — de-dup (qa F-T2-1 round-15):** two `_enqueueReadd(playerId)` calls in one tick result in the member being present in `aliveMembers` exactly once with no error (the append is idempotent per `{playerId}` — `aliveMembers[playerId] = true` twice is a no-op, mirroring the `_enqueueDeparture` de-dup), and `_pendingReadds` holds at most one entry for that player. A member who died pre-BC4 and did NOT respawn is never resurrected. This part is fully deterministic via the C.16 alive-state seams and IS the sprint-exit gate.
  - **Part 2 — Deferred (Integration, ADVISORY, unverified-until-PC):** that the **canonical** re-add flows from PC's `OnSquadMemberAliveChanged(playerId, isAlive)` (which supersedes the interim path) — **unverified until PC publishes the signal** (PC in MAJOR REVISION; F.4 BLOCKING obligation), NOT a sprint-exit blocker for the Crafting sprint.
- **Test type**: Part 1 Logic (sprint-gate, via C.16 `_enqueueDeparture` + alive-state seams); Part 2 Integration (**unverified-until-PC** — PC `OnSquadMemberAliveChanged`, F.4)
- **Source**: C.8 whole-run enqueue (death/disconnect reason-gated), C.10 `aliveMembers` / `_pendingDepartures` / interim re-add, C.9, F.4 PC `OnSquadMemberAliveChanged` (BLOCKING)

**H.109 [P0] — In-window run-end cleanup runs EXACTLY ONCE across the Crafting drain + PC's all-dead path (Convergence #2)**
- **GIVEN** the beacon is in BC4; the last living member departs, so both (a) Crafting's buffered drain would fire `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="wipe")` on the BC4 tick AND (b) PC's generic all-dead `RunEnded(defeat)` path could fire synchronously from the same `Humanoid.Died`; the test drives the same-tick race via the C.16 seam
- **WHEN** the terminal transition is resolved
- **THEN** run-end cleanup executes **exactly once**: `RunSession.runOutcomeResolved` is set by whichever path reaches it first, and the second path is a **no-op** (the BC4 handler returns immediately when `runOutcomeResolved` is already true; PC suppresses its all-dead path entirely for the whole post-activation lifecycle — gated on `beaconActivated == true` **alone** (build-gate A4: the narrower `beaconActivated && !beaconWindowSurvived` form released suppression at the victory write and reopened a post-victory defeat-race; round-22 game B3 corrected this AC to the canonical whole-lifecycle gate of C.9/C.12.1/F.4), the primary guard — C.9/C.12.1); the Beacon BasePart is torn down once, timers cleared once, and no second `RunEnded`/cleanup fires. This is the "second terminal path is a no-op" AC the round-10 qa lens (BLOCK-2) required
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: C.9 BCT-DEFEAT mutual-exclusion + `runOutcomeResolved`, C.10 latch invariant, C.12.1 PC BC4 suppression

**H.110 [P0] — `_enqueueDeparture` is wired to the real `Humanoid.Died` / `Players.PlayerRemoving` connections (seam-integrity, no production divergence)**
- **GIVEN** the production `CraftingService` wiring (not a test double): the real `Humanoid.Died` and `Players.PlayerRemoving` event connections, and the 5 Hz proximity watcher's out-of-range path
- **WHEN** each of those production signals fires for a member who is a crafter and/or a BC4 window participant
- **THEN** each routes through the **same** `_enqueueDeparture(playerId, reason)` seam the tests drive (C.8/C.16) — i.e. the test seam and the production event path are the *same* code, so a green C.16-driven departure test (H.99/H.101/H.103/H.104/H.108) is evidence about production behavior, not just about the seam. A reviewer/static check confirms **no production path mutates `crafters` / `aliveMembers` *directly* at all** — every **removal** goes through `_enqueueDeparture` and (round-13 build-gate fix) every **re-add** goes through `_enqueueReadd`, both drained inside `_step` (departures then re-adds, C.8 ordering). This closes the round-13-flagged asymmetry where the respawn re-add was a *direct* `CharacterAdded` callback write racing the buffered departure drain (the round-11 "sanctioned direct add" exemption is **retired** — the re-add is now buffered like every other alive-set mutation, so the "no direct mutation" rule is universal, not scoped only to removals). The re-add enqueue still fires only after the respawned `HumanoidRootPart` is positioned (build-gate A3), so the queue never holds an unplaced member
- **Test type**: Logic (static/integration — connection-wiring assertion that the real events call only `_enqueueDeparture`)
- **Source**: C.8 `_enqueueDeparture` fan-out, C.16 seam table, R4-5 buffered departures

**H.111 — The three pulsing Crafting BC4 surfaces ship a non-pulsing reduced-motion variant (UX-B2)**
- **GIVEN** the player's OS/Roblox reduced-motion preference is enabled (`UserGameSettings.ReducedMotion`); the beacon is in BC4 rendering the survival-window countdown-ring, the `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` world-ring, and (if a Coil is active) the final-10s warning, plus the Conv #5 escalation cue
- **WHEN** each surface renders under reduced-motion
- **THEN** each uses a **non-pulsing static variant** that still conveys its state (remaining time, in/out-of-radius, expiry-imminent, escalation urgency) without animated pulsing — reduced-motion *removes* the pulse, distinct from H.95 which only caps the flash *rate* at default motion. (Round-10 ux UX-B2: H.95 alone never affirmatively required a reduced-motion path.)
- **Test type**: UI / Config-Data (advisory — reduced-motion-flag render assertion or review checklist)
- **Source**: UI.5 item 6, UI.1 surface 13 (escalation), WCAG 2.3.3 / reduced-motion

**H.112 [P0] — A bench-only departure (cancel / out-of-bench-range) does NOT drain `aliveMembers`; only death/disconnect does (build-gate C3)**
- **GIVEN** the beacon is in BC4 with an alive squad; via the C.16 seam a member who is **alive** fires `RequestCraftCancel` (`reason="player-initiated"`), and in a second variant the 5 Hz proximity watcher flags them out-of-**bench**-range — both while they remain alive and within `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS`
- **WHEN** `_enqueueDeparture` fans the departure out (C.8)
- **THEN** the departure is appended to the **bench** `CraftSession._pendingDepartures` scope only (firing BT4/BT5 as applicable) and is **NOT** appended to `RunSession._pendingDepartures`, so the member is **NOT removed from `aliveMembers`** and the window-end `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot still counts them — no spurious wipe/scatter. The complementary positive: a `death`/`disconnect` reason **does** drain `aliveMembers`. Only `reason ∈ {"death","disconnect"}` reaches the alive scope
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_enqueueDeparture` seam)
- **Source**: C.8 reason-gated fan-out, C.10 `_pendingDepartures` scopes

**H.113 [P0] — A run that ends outside BC4 (PC all-dead pre-activation, or any `RunEnded`) latches `runOutcomeResolved` and halts the whole-run drain (build-gate C2)**
- **GIVEN** a run with no beacon activated yet (or otherwise outside BC4); via the seam the run ends through PC's all-dead `RunEnded(defeat)` path (or any `RunEnded` source)
- **WHEN** `CraftingService` receives the `RunEnded` broadcast
- **THEN** Crafting's `RunEnded` subscription sets `RunSession.runOutcomeResolved = true` and **ceases all `_step` run-scope work** (the bench + alive drains stop on subsequent ticks); the whole-run `aliveMembers` drain does not keep running against an ended run; no Crafting window signal (`OnBeaconWindowSurvived`/`OnBeaconWindowFailed`) fires (the run did not end via a BC4 outcome). PC owns the `RunEnded` broadcast; Crafting does not depend on PC writing the Crafting-owned latch
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: C.10 `runOutcomeResolved` (`RunEnded` subscription), C.8 whole-run drain, C.12

### H.114–H.117 — Round-13 Additions (continuous-hold line-break + grace, payload propagation, dampener cap)

Added by the round-13 authoring pass (2026-06-04) for the KITING fail-fast continuous-hold ruling + the round-12 convergences/single-lens AC gaps. H.114 (line-break grace mechanics), H.115 (wipe-immediate vs scatter-grace), H.116 (`windowDurationSeconds` payload — Conv-2), H.117 (Dampener interim 0.56 cap — qa). Numbers append at H.114+; none reuse a retired number.

**H.114 [P0] — Continuous line-break grace: a below-threshold in-radius snapshot for the full grace fires scatter-defeat; re-entry within grace resets it (B1 round-13, holder-threshold round-15)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 with a 4-member squad (`requiredHolders = 2`); via the C.16 `_clock`/`_step`/`_setMemberPosition` seams the test drives the in-radius count **below `requiredHolders`** (e.g. 2→1 in radius) while `aliveMembers` stays non-empty
- **WHEN** the BC4 handler evaluates the line-break path (C.9 step b/c) across successive `_step`s
- **THEN** three sub-cases, all deterministic via the seam:
  - **(a) grace elapses → defeat:** the first below-threshold tick stamps `_lineBreakSince = _clock()`; if the snapshot stays below threshold until `_clock() - _lineBreakSince >= BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`, BCT-DEFEAT fires `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="scatter")` **exactly once** (computed in step b, fired in step c after the drains, gated on `aliveMembers` non-empty — B-1) and sets `runOutcomeResolved`;
  - **(b) re-entry resets:** if enough alive members re-enter the radius (back to `>= requiredHolders`) before the grace elapses, `_lineBreakSince` resets to `nil`, **NO** defeat fires, and a later break starts a fresh full grace;
  - **(c) jitter/knockback under grace fires zero:** a member pushed out and back (count below then back to `>= requiredHolders`) within `< BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` fires zero defeats — the run continues.
- The `OnBeaconLineBreak(broken, timestamp, graceSeconds)` HUD signal fires `broken=true` immediately on the first below-threshold tick and `broken=false` only after `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE` consecutive at-threshold ticks (round-15 restore debounce, C.9 step d / C.10 `_lineBreakRestoreTicks`); presentation only — it does not gate the terminal (which the strict `_lineBreakSince` grace owns).
- **(d) client-independence (round-13 network gate):** the server fires `OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="scatter")` at grace expiry **regardless of whether the client ever received or rendered `OnBeaconLineBreak`** — a test that suppresses/drops the client-side signal asserts the server still resolves the terminal from its own `_lineBreakSince` grace clock (the client regroup countdown is cosmetic; the terminal is server-only — fail-closed authority).
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_clock`/`_step`/`_setMemberPosition`)
- **Source**: C.5.6a, C.9 step b, C.10 `_lineBreakSince`, G.6 `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`, E.27

**H.115 [P0] — Wipe is immediate (no grace); a displacement line-break uses the grace (round-13)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; two variants via the C.16 seam — (a) the last alive members all die/disconnect on a tick so the drain empties `aliveMembers`; (b) the last in-radius members all leave the radius but stay alive
- **WHEN** the BC4 handler resolves each
- **THEN** variant (a) **wipe**: the drain (C.9 step c) empties `aliveMembers` and BCT-DEFEAT(`reason="wipe"`) fires **immediately on that tick — no grace consulted** (`_lineBreakSince` is not awaited); variant (b) **scatter**: BCT-DEFEAT(`reason="scatter"`) fires only after the full `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` (H.114). **Composite:** a squad in an in-progress scatter grace whose members then all die resolves as **wipe immediately** — the wipe path (alive-set empty, step c) takes precedence over the running grace
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: C.9 BCT-DEFEAT (wipe immediate vs scatter grace), C.10, E.24, E.27

**H.116 [P0] — Activation signals carry `windowDurationSeconds == BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW` (Conv-2 round-13)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon reaches BC3 and BCT3 fires (via the C.16 seam, `_clock()` controlled)
- **WHEN** the BCT3 side-effects fire the activation signals
- **THEN** both `OnBeaconActivated(position, activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` and `OnEscapeBeaconActivated(activatorPlayerId, timestamp, windowDurationSeconds)` carry `windowDurationSeconds == BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW` (the server-derived window length) and `timestamp == _clock()` at activation; **neither payload is truncated** (the round-12 Conv-2 miss: C.9 BCT3 side-effects + C.12.1 + UI.3 omitted `windowDurationSeconds`, leaving the client unable to derive window-end). A client computing `windowEnd = timestamp + windowDurationSeconds` recovers the exact countdown the server uses, and the same value drives the surface-13 escalation
- **Test type**: Logic (payload assertion via C.16)
- **Source**: C.9 BCT3, C.12.1, C.14, C.15, UI.3, G.6

**H.117 — DampenerCoil `DAMPENER_REDUCTION` is held at the interim 0.56 ceiling until PC's Stage-2 lands (qa round-13)**
- **GIVEN** `CraftingService` config with a `DAMPENER_REDUCTION` value; the D.6a Stage-2 PC stationary factor is unverified-until-PC (PC in MAJOR REVISION)
- **WHEN** the startup config-validation runs
- **THEN** a `DAMPENER_REDUCTION` in `[0.30, 0.56]` passes; a value `> 0.56` (the **interim** ceiling, round-11 systems BLOCK-3, D.6/G.5) is **rejected** as out-of-band — the 0.70 post-PC ceiling is NOT yet permitted, because a moving Coil player at 0.70 emits 0.024 (near-silent *only if* PC's Stage-2 stacks, which is unconfirmed), so the silencing line cannot reach a live run before PC verifies Stage-2. Mirrors the H.82/H.105 fail-fast-config pattern. (When PC lands and D.6a Stage-2 is verified, the ceiling lifts to 0.70 and this AC's bound updates.)
- **Test type**: Config-Data / Logic
- **Source**: D.6, D.6a (Stage-2 unverified-until-PC), G.5 interim cap

### H.118–H.121 — Round-15 Additions (holder threshold, restore debounce, break-recover-win, mobile recoverability)

Added by the round-15 authoring pass (2026-06-05) for the SENTRY fix (`requiredHolders`), the quadruple-lens restore debounce, and the two missing qa ACs. Numbers append at H.118+; none reuse a retired number.

**H.118 [P0] — A respawn during BC4 never raises `requiredHolders` above the frozen baseline; the frozen baseline never relaxes below itself on death (E.33 sub-case A, round-16 R16-1 freeze; round-19 R18-1 peak-source)**
- **GIVEN** two variants via the C.16 `_enqueueReadd`/`_setMemberPosition`/`_clock`/`_step` seams. **The baseline source is `peakAliveCount` (round-19 R18-1), so each variant's squad is described by its run high-water.** **(a) additive-respawn case** — a squad whose high-water is **2** (`peakAliveCount = 2`, so `requiredHoldersBaseline = ceil(2/2) = 1`): both alive at activation, exactly **1** in radius (`requiredHolders = min(1, 2) = 1`, held); one dies (`aliveMembers = 1`, `min(1,1)=1`, lone holds), then respawns (`aliveMembers = 2`). **(b) freeze-discriminating restore case** — a squad whose high-water is **4** (`peakAliveCount = 4`, `requiredHoldersBaseline = 2`): drops to **1** alive mid-window (`requiredHolders = min(2, 1) = 1`, the lone holder suffices), then a teammate respawns to **2** alive
- **WHEN** the **next** BC4 `_step` evaluates the hold against `requiredHolders = min(requiredHoldersBaseline, #aliveMembers)`
- **THEN** in variant (a): `requiredHolders` stays `min(1, 2) = 1` — the respawn is **purely additive** (a body that can help hold), it **does NOT raise the bar to 2**; `_lineBreakSince` does NOT stamp from the respawn. In variant (b): `requiredHolders` goes `min(2, 2) = 2` — the respawn restores the requirement **toward (never above)** the frozen baseline of 2; **critically, at 2 alive the frozen baseline holds the bar at 2 even though a live per-tick recompute (`ceil(2/2) = 1`) would have relaxed it** — this discriminates the freeze (deaths never relax the bar below the frozen baseline; respawns never raise it above). If the held count is now short, the normal `_lineBreakSince` grace applies (recoverable in the 12-stud radius). Asserts `requiredHoldersBaseline` is read once at BCT3 and never recomputed; the per-tick `min` only clamps down to current alive.
- **Note**: the baseline is `ceil(peakAliveCount/2)`, frozen at BCT3 (round-19 R18-1 — the max-alive-so-far ratchet replaced the round-16 activation-snapshot source, closing the activate-late lane; H.129 covers that). The denominator-source decision is locked, not a pending swap.
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_enqueueReadd` + `_setMemberPosition` + `_clock`/`_step`)
- **Source**: E.33, C.9 step b/c, BCT3 step 6, C.10 `requiredHoldersBaseline` / `requiredHolders` / `_pendingReadds`

**H.119 [P0] — `OnBeaconLineBreak` is edge-triggered both ways (broken=true once-per-episode round-16 R16-2; broken=false debounced round-15); a boundary-oscillating sole holder does not flap the win-state banner (quadruple-lens)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; via the C.16 `_setMemberPosition`/`_step` seams the test drives the in-radius count across the `requiredHolders` threshold and back **on alternating ticks** (a sole boundary-holder oscillating at the 12-stud radius)
- **WHEN** the BC4 handler runs step (d) across those ticks
- **THEN** `OnBeaconLineBreak(broken=true)` fires **exactly once** — on the **first** below-threshold tick (the HOLDING→BROKEN edge), because it is **edge-triggered off `RunSession._lineBreakBroadcast`** (round-16 R16-2): it fires only when `_lineBreakBroadcast` was `false` and then sets it `true`, so the **subsequent** below-threshold ticks of the oscillation (which keep dipping below threshold) find `_lineBreakBroadcast` already `true` and do **NOT** re-fire (the round-15 spec, lacking this guard, would have re-fired `broken=true` on every below-threshold tick — the systems-B2/qa-B1 AC-vs-spec contradiction this AC now passes against the fixed spec); `broken=false` fires **only** once `_lineBreakRestoreTicks` reaches `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE` (G.6, 6 consecutive at-threshold ticks) AND `_lineBreakBroadcast` is `true` — so an oscillation that never sustains the threshold for the full debounce fires **exactly one** `broken=true` and **zero** `broken=false`, NOT a per-tick true/false flap; an oscillation that does sustain N at-threshold ticks fires exactly one `broken=false`. Asserts the banner transition count is bounded by the edge-trigger + debounce, not by the oscillation frequency (the WCAG 2.3.1 / N14-B2 flap foreclosure, H.95). The terminal `_lineBreakSince` grace clock is unaffected (it uses the strict per-tick threshold).
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: C.9 step d, C.10 `_lineBreakRestoreTicks` / `_lineBreakBroadcast`, C.14 `OnBeaconLineBreak`, G.6 `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE`, H.95

**H.120 [P0] — Break-within-grace → recover → subsequently WIN (the path the grace exists for — qa F-T3-1 round-15)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 with members alive; via the C.16 seam the in-radius count drops **below `requiredHolders`** mid-window (line-break grace starts), then returns to `>= requiredHolders` **before** the grace elapses (`_lineBreakSince` resets to `nil`), and the squad then **holds continuously** until `_clock()` reaches `beaconActivationTime + BEACON_SURVIVAL_WINDOW`
- **WHEN** the window-end `_step` evaluates window-survival (C.9 step a) with the hold re-established
- **THEN** BCT4 fires → `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` → **victory** — the break that recovered within grace did NOT prevent the win, and `OnBeaconWindowFailed` never fired. This is the exact scenario the grace exists to permit; an implementation that reset the grace clock but still latched a pending scatter at window-end would FAIL this AC (catching the round-14 qa F-T3-1 gap, where a "reset clock but still fire scatter" impl would pass the prior suite).
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_clock`/`_step`/`_setMemberPosition`)
- **Source**: C.5.6a, C.9 step a/b, C.10 `_lineBreakSince`, H.114

**H.121 — Mobile recoverability is a JOINT inequality over radius, grace, MoveSpeed, touch latency, and the PA knockback cap — NOT a radius-edge assertion (ux F1 round-15; round-18 ux B-1 / qa B-2 substantiation fix)**
- **GIVEN** the locked tunings `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS = 12 studs` and `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE = 3 s`; the lowest-tier-mobile reaction budget `t_react` (perception ~0.3–0.8 s + orientation ~0.3–0.6 s ≈ **1.1–1.4 s**); the **mobile touch input latency** `t_latency` (the tap-to-move-start delay on a low-tier touch device, ~0.1–0.2 s, omitted by the round-15 model); the player's `MoveSpeed` (PC-owned, **unverified-until-PC**); and the **worst-case single-event outward displacement** `PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK` (PA-owned, **unverified-until-PA**, F.4 PA row) — because **most line-breaks are CAUSED by a knockback that pushes a holder OUTWARD**, the displacement to recover is **not** the 12-stud radius edge (the round-15 model's false assumption — round-18 ux B-1) but the distance the knockback throws the member past the radius
- **WHEN** the recoverability check evaluates the joint inequality for a holder displaced by the maximal BC4 knockback to perceive BROKEN, orient (incl. touch latency), and traverse back inside `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` at `MoveSpeed`
- **THEN** the binding condition is **`t_react + t_latency + (displacement_to_re-enter / MoveSpeed) < BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`**, where `displacement_to_re-enter` is bounded by `PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK` (PA caps how far a single BC4 event can throw a held member). This is a **joint constraint over {radius, grace, MoveSpeed, touch_latency, PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK}** — it is **NOT closed by the radius alone**, and the round-15 "≤12-stud traverse ≈ 0.7 s, recoverable" claim was **unsubstantiated** because it assumed the holder sits at the radius edge rather than wherever a 20–30-stud knockback throws them (round-18 B-1/B-2). **The round-14 game-vs-ux grace disagreement is therefore resolved by PA *bounding the knockback* (so worst-case displacement is traversable within the 3 s grace minus the reaction+latency budget) — NOT by stretching the grace past its ~4 s cap.** Worked envelope at an illustrative `MoveSpeed ≈ 16 studs/s`: `(3 − 1.4 − 0.2) × 16 ≈ 22 studs` of total traverse budget, so the PA cap must keep a holder's post-knockback distance-to-re-enter at or under ~22 studs (with margin, less) — the falsifiable target PA owes (F.4 PA row, `PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK`). **MVP status: this is an Integration forward obligation, NOT an advisory prose assertion** — it becomes a checkable joint-inequality AC when PA publishes `PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK` and PC publishes `MoveSpeed`. If those values cannot satisfy the inequality, the dials (radius / grace / knockback cap) must be re-balanced jointly — the grace stays ≤ 4 s, the knockback cap absorbs the difference.
- **Test type**: Integration (joint tuning-relationship; unverified-until PA `PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK` + PC `MoveSpeed`)
- **Source**: G.6 `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` / `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`, C.5.6a, F.4 PA row (`PREDATOR_BC4_MAX_KNOCKBACK`), ux B-1 / qa B-2 round-18, PC `MoveSpeed` (unverified-until-PC)

### H.122–H.124, H.129 — Round-16/19 Additions (run-end cleanup sweep, re-add predicate, baseline enumeration, activate-late ratchet)

Added by the round-17 authoring pass (2026-06-06) closing round-16 gaps: H.122 (`_runEndCleanup` sweeps `_craftSessions` — round-12 REC-5 / round-16 network recommended), H.123 (the `POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON` re-add predicate — round-16 qa B3, the only round-15 rule with a named seam and no AC), H.124 (`requiredHoldersBaseline` enumeration — round-16 qa recommended, restated round-19 to the peak-source + concrete respawn THEN). **H.129 (round-19 R18-1 — activate-late ratchet) is grouped here with H.118/H.124 as the third baseline-edge AC.** Numbers append; none reuse a retired number.

**H.123 [P0] — The `POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON` re-add predicate gates `_enqueueReadd` only after the respawned root part is positioned (round-16 qa B3)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; a member is dead (absent from `aliveMembers`); via the C.16 `_setMemberPosition`/`_step` seams the test models that member's respawn in two sub-cases — (a) the freshly-parented `HumanoidRootPart` reads world origin (`Position.Magnitude <= POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON`, the unplaced state); (b) the root part is then placed at a `SpawnLocation` well away from origin (`Position.Magnitude > POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON`)
- **WHEN** the server-side re-add path evaluates the positioned predicate `hrp ~= nil AND hrp.Position.Magnitude > POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON` (G.6) on each `_step`
- **THEN** in sub-case (a) `_enqueueReadd` is **NOT** called and the member stays absent from `aliveMembers` (so the `windowAliveInRadius` read never observes an unplaced root part at origin); in sub-case (b) `_enqueueReadd(playerId)` is called **exactly once** (the per-step check then unsubscribes for that respawn), the re-add drains in `_step` step (c), and the member re-enters `aliveMembers` cleanly. Asserts the round-15 "positioned" predicate is a single testable boolean (build-gate A3 / B-3) and is the only gate on the buffered re-add.
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_setMemberPosition` + `_step`)
- **Source**: C.10 `_pendingReadds` / `_enqueueReadd`, G.6 `POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON`, C.16 `_enqueueReadd` seam

**H.124 — `requiredHoldersBaseline` enumerates `{1,1,2,2}` for high-water squad sizes `{1,2,3,4}` and is frozen (round-16 R16-1; round-19 R18-1 peak-source)**
- **GIVEN** four BCT3 activation variants via the C.16 seam in which **no one died before activation**, so `peakAliveCount == #aliveMembers` at activation `∈ {1, 2, 3, 4}`
- **WHEN** BCT3 step 6 computes `requiredHoldersBaseline = ceil(peakAliveCount / 2)` and stores it; then, in the **size-4 variant**, a member dies (`aliveMembers = 3`) and on a later tick respawns (`aliveMembers = 4`)
- **THEN** `requiredHoldersBaseline` is `{1, 1, 2, 2}` respectively, is **stored once and not recomputed** on any later tick. **Concrete respawn THEN (size-4 variant, qa straddle fix — stated inline, not by cross-ref):** when the member dies to 3 alive, `requiredHoldersBaseline` is **unchanged at 2** and `requiredHolders = min(2, 3) = 2`; when they respawn to 4 alive, `requiredHoldersBaseline` is **still 2** (the stored field is not rewritten) and `requiredHolders = min(2, 4) = 2` — the respawn does not raise the baseline or the per-tick value above 2. Asserts the frozen enumeration and that neither a death nor a respawn rewrites the stored baseline.
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: C.9 BCT3 step 6, C.10 `peakAliveCount` / `requiredHoldersBaseline`, G.6 `BEACON_HOLD_MIN_HOLDERS`

**H.129 [P0] — Activating late never lowers the holder baseline: `requiredHoldersBaseline` reads the run high-water, not the activation snapshot (E.33 sub-case B, round-19 R18-1 ratchet)**
- **GIVEN** a squad that started with **4** members (`peakAliveCount = 4` from RunStarted); via the C.16 `_enqueueDeparture`/`_clock`/`_step` seams, **two members die BEFORE BCT3** (drained by the run-scope drain pre-activation, so `#aliveMembers = 2` at activation), then the Beacon is activated. A complementary variant activates **immediately at the full 4** (no pre-activation deaths).
- **WHEN** BCT3 step 6 computes `requiredHoldersBaseline = ceil(peakAliveCount / 2)`
- **THEN** in **both** variants `requiredHoldersBaseline = ceil(4 / 2) = 2` — the late-activation variant does **NOT** get the lower `ceil(2/2) = 1` it would under the retired round-16 activation-snapshot rule, because `peakAliveCount` was ratcheted to 4 at RunStarted and a pre-activation death never lowers it (`max`-ratchet). So **delaying activation until teammates die buys no reduction in the holder bar** (the round-18 "activate-late-for-low-baseline" degenerate lane is closed), and **activation timing is irrelevant to the baseline** (both variants get 2). The per-tick `requiredHolders = min(2, #aliveMembers)` still clamps down to current alive (a lone survivor among the two activates `min(2,1)=1` and can win). Asserts the ratchet source and the lane closure.
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_enqueueDeparture` + `_clock`/`_step`)
- **Source**: C.9 BCT3 step 6, C.10 `peakAliveCount` / `requiredHoldersBaseline`, E.33 sub-case B, G.6 `BEACON_HOLD_MIN_HOLDERS`

**H.122 — `_runEndCleanup()` sweeps `_craftSessions` and all runtime state on any run end (round-16 / round-12 REC-5)**
- **GIVEN** a run with at least one active `CraftSession` in `_craftSessions` (a bench mid-craft) AND (a sub-case) the beacon in BC4; the run then ends via any path — BCT4 victory, BCT-DEFEAT, BCT5 decay, BindToClose, or a PC-owned outside-BC4 `RunEnded` (via Crafting's `RunEnded` subscription)
- **WHEN** `_runEndCleanup()` runs (the named centralised cleanup, C.10)
- **THEN** `_craftSessions` is emptied (in-progress sessions abandoned without firing BT3/BT5 — no item granted, no Craft emission), the window-scoped fields are reset (`_windowPrevInRadius`/`_lineBreakSince`/`_lineBreakRestoreTicks`/`_lineBreakBroadcast`/`requiredHoldersBaseline` per H.94), and the runtime inventories/deployables/equipped-effects are cleared — so no bench progress-tick or emission can run against an ended run. (Closes round-12 REC-5 + the round-16 network "`_craftSessions` RunEnded sweep" recommended.)
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_simulateRestart`/`_simulateBindToClose`/`_enqueueDeparture` + `RunEnded`)
- **Source**: C.10 `_runEndCleanup` / `_craftSessions`, E.8 run-end suppression

### H.125–H.128 — Round-19 Additions (graceSeconds restore payload, position-spoof velocity check, scatter-vs-wipe precedence, reconnect-during-BC4)

Added by the round-19 authoring pass (2026-06-06) closing the round-18 verdict: H.125 (`graceSeconds = 0` on the `broken=false` payload — R18-4 / network N18-B2), H.126 (post-MVP position-delta velocity check — R18-3 / network N18-B1 forward obligation), H.127 (same-tick scatter-grace-expiry-AND-wipe precedence — qa recommended), H.128 (reconnect-during-BC4 end-to-end — qa recommended). H.129 (activate-late ratchet) is grouped with the baseline ACs above. Numbers append; none reuse a retired number.

**H.125 [P0] — `OnBeaconLineBreak(broken=false)` carries `graceSeconds = 0` server-side (R18-4, network N18-B2)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; via the C.16 seam the test drives a line-break (`broken=true` fires with `graceSeconds = BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`) then a full recovery (≥ `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_RESTORE_DEBOUNCE` consecutive at-threshold ticks) so the restore edge fires
- **WHEN** the server emits the `OnBeaconLineBreak(broken=false)` restore edge (C.9 step d / C.14)
- **THEN** the payload's `graceSeconds` field is **`0`**, set server-side — NOT the live `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` and NOT a stale value the client is expected to ignore. The grace is already resolved at re-establish time, so a `0` is the unambiguous server-authoritative signal; **the round-16 "the client IGNORES it" parenthetical is removed** (the HUD GDD must not be relied on to discard a non-zero value — round-18 N18-B2). The `broken=true` payload still carries `graceSeconds = BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`.
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16)
- **Source**: C.9 step d, C.14 `OnBeaconLineBreak`, G.6 `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE`, R18-4

**H.126 — Post-MVP position-delta velocity sanity check rejects a teleport across `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` (forward obligation, R18-3 / network N18-B1)**
- **GIVEN** *(this AC is a forward obligation — Integration, unverified-until the anti-cheat/movement hardening pass; the MVP fences this risk per E.34 and does NOT build it now)* a future server-side movement check with a `MAX_PLAYER_SPEED` ceiling; Beacon is in BC4; via the C.16 `_setMemberPosition` seam the test sets a member's position to one outside `BEACON_HOLD_RADIUS` on tick *t* and snaps it to inside the radius on tick *t+1*, an implied speed far above `MAX_PLAYER_SPEED`
- **WHEN** the (post-MVP) velocity sanity check evaluates the `_memberPosition` delta between ticks
- **THEN** the implausible position update is **ignored/rejected** for the hold check (the member is not counted as in-radius from a teleport), so a client cannot spoof itself into `requiredHolders` (forge-win) or out of it (grief-line-break) by snapping its replicated `HumanoidRootPart.Position` across the boundary faster than a real character could traverse it. A legitimate at-`MoveSpeed` traversal (implied speed ≤ `MAX_PLAYER_SPEED`) is accepted normally. **MVP status: NOT IMPLEMENTED — fenced (E.34); this AC defines the acceptance contract for when the hardening pass lands.**
- **Test type**: Integration (unverified-until anti-cheat pass)
- **Source**: E.34, F.5 (position-replication caveat), C.10 `_memberPosition` / C.16 seam, R18-3

**H.127 [P0] — Same tick: scatter grace expires AND the squad wipes → resolves as WIPE, not scatter (B-1 precedence)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 with a line-break in progress (`_lineBreakSince` stamped, members alive but `#windowAliveInRadius < requiredHolders`); via the C.16 seam, on a single `_step` the grace clock reaches `BEACON_LINE_BREAK_GRACE` (so step (b) computes `graceElapsed = true`) **AND** that same tick's buffered departures (drained at step c) empty `RunSession.aliveMembers`
- **WHEN** the BC4 handler resolves the terminal at step (c) (C.9 — wipe checked before scatter)
- **THEN** the run resolves as **`OnBeaconWindowFailed(reason="wipe")`**, NOT `reason="scatter"` — the wipe check (`aliveMembers` empty after both drains) is evaluated **first** and takes precedence over the same-tick scatter `graceElapsed` (B-1 round-15: step (b) is compute-only; the scatter terminal fires in step (c) only if `aliveMembers` is still non-empty after the drains).
