Exactly one terminal fires; `OnBeaconWindowSurvived` does not fire; the precedence is deterministic via the single-handler ordering (no scheduler race). *(This is the standalone same-tick precedence AC the round-18 qa lens requested — H.89 covers the three outcomes in isolation; this pins the wipe-wins-the-tie ordering when grace-expiry and wipe land on the same Heartbeat.)*
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_clock`/`_step`/`_setMemberPosition`/`_enqueueDeparture`)
- **Source**: C.9 step (b)/(c) wipe-before-scatter, E.24 note, E.27, H.89, H.115

**H.128 — Reconnect during BC4: a member who disconnects and reconnects mid-window re-enters the alive-set cleanly (qa recommended; mobile backgrounding)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4; via the C.16 seam a member **disconnects** mid-window (`Players.PlayerRemoving` → `_enqueueDeparture(playerId, "disconnect")`, drained step c → removed from `aliveMembers`), then **reconnects** and respawns (modelling mobile app-backgrounding then foregrounding): their character re-parents, the positioned predicate `hrp.Position.Magnitude > POSITION_SETTLED_EPSILON` (H.123) holds, and `_enqueueReadd(playerId)` fires
- **WHEN** the subsequent `_step` drains `_pendingReadds` (after `_pendingDepartures`)
- **THEN** the reconnecting member is **re-added** to `aliveMembers` (idempotent), re-enters the `windowAliveInRadius` snapshot once positioned-and-in-radius, and counts toward `requiredHolders` again — exactly as a respawn does (E.33 sub-case A / H.118). The disconnect→reconnect round-trip nets correctly: while disconnected they are absent from the hold count (and `requiredHolders` clamps down via `min(baseline, #aliveMembers)`); on reconnect the bar restores toward (never above) the frozen baseline. `peakAliveCount` is unchanged by the round-trip (the `max` ratchet never lowers). No wipe is falsely triggered if the reconnect drains the same tick as another member's death (drain-after-departures ordering, C.9 step c).
- **Test type**: Logic (via C.16 `_enqueueDeparture` + `_enqueueReadd` + `_setMemberPosition`/`_step`)
- **Source**: C.10 `aliveMembers` / `_pendingDepartures` / `_pendingReadds` / `peakAliveCount`, C.9 step c, E.33 sub-case A, H.118, H.123

### H.130 — Round-20 Addition (denominator-change cue)

Added by the round-20 closure pass (2026-06-06) closing the round-20 ux UX-B1 finding: a player-facing cue must fire whenever the `N/M holding` denominator `M = requiredHolders` changes mid-window, so the shifting win-target is never silent. Number appends; does not reuse a retired number.

**H.130 — A change to `requiredHolders` mid-window emits a client-derivable denominator-change cue naming the new value and its cause (ux UX-B1)**
- **GIVEN** Beacon is in BC4 with a 3-player squad (`requiredHoldersBaseline = ceil(peakAliveCount/2) = 2`, so `M = 2`, surface 13 shows `N/2 holding`); via the C.16 seam the test (i) drives a member death (`_enqueueDeparture` → `#aliveMembers` 3→2 → `requiredHolders = min(2, 2) = 2`; no change) then a second death (`#aliveMembers` 2→1 → `requiredHolders = min(2, 1) = 1`, `M` drops 2→1), then (ii) drives a respawn (`_enqueueReadd` → `#aliveMembers` 1→2 → `requiredHolders = min(2, 2) = 2`, `M` rises 1→2)
- **WHEN** the client renders surface 13 across the `M`-change ticks (the cue is presentation-only and client-derived from `#aliveMembers` + the pushed `requiredHolders`; no new server signal)
- **THEN** on the `M` 2→1 edge a transient cue fires naming the **new requirement and its cause** (text + a non-color encoding — e.g. "HOLDERS NEEDED: 1 ▼ — a teammate fell"), and on the `M` 1→2 edge a corresponding cue fires ("HOLDERS NEEDED: 2 ▲ — a teammate is back"); a tick with **no** `M`-change fires **no** cue. The cue **does not** change the C.9 window evaluation (presentation-only), respects the WCAG ≤ 3 flashes/s cap (H.95) and the reduced-motion requirement (H.111), and `M` never exceeds the frozen `requiredHoldersBaseline` (the respawn restores toward, never above, baseline — E.33 / H.118).
- **Test type**: Logic (cue-emission edge via C.16 `_enqueueDeparture`/`_enqueueReadd`/`_step`; the visual treatment itself is a `/ux-design` deliverable — Visual/Feel, advisory)
- **Source**: UI.1 surface 13 (denominator-change cue), E.33, H.118, H.95, H.111, UI.4 colorblind rule, round-20 ux UX-B1

In the *dominant oxygen-heavy line* — stack Oxygen Canisters (BIOMASS) for the BC4 drain + the one Beacon — the squad crafts few Coils/Anchors, so **MINERAL and especially RESONANT see thin practical per-run demand** (RESONANT's only non-Beacon consumer is the Coil). The BIOMASS/MINERAL practical-demand half remains a playtest-tuning question. **Fenced** per the round-8/round-9 CD direction for the tuning half: not a re-litigated Crafting blocker (the structural consumer exists; practical balance closes when RM/PA land). Exact catalog weights remain a playtest-tuning question. | Tuning / Economy | economy-designer | Pre-Production playtest sprint 1 (with OQ.13) |

### Resolved during this round (logged for traceability)

The following game-concept / ED-owned questions were resolved by this GDD. They are NOT open — listed only to confirm the obligations have been met:

- **ED.Q1 / Q2** (Beacon `initialMagnitude` exact value) — RESOLVED at `MAGNITUDE_BEACON = 0.95` per ED.C.3.6 default, registered in ED. Documented in C.2 row 7 + C.5.4 + this GDD's H.14.
- **ED.Q4** (whether crafted items beyond Beacon publish their own emission types) — RESOLVED: a single new `Craft` emission type is added to ED's reserved 4-slot enum (consumes 1 slot; 3 reserved slots remain for future post-launch content). All MVP non-Beacon Crafting bursts use this single `Craft` type with magnitude tier per VA.1.
- **Game-concept open question** (Crafting tree purpose: 4–8 items beyond the win-condition pair) — RESOLVED: **3 aid items** defined (Oxygen Canister + Dampener Coil + Signal Anchor), all consumable / time-limited, all respecting cosmetic-boundary rule, all serving survival aid / disturbance reduction / coordination roles per Section B. *(Round-2, 2026-06-01: cut from 6 — the Composite Patch and Quiet Step Wrap were removed as trap recipes. Round-5, 2026-06-02: cut from 4 — the Squad Relay was removed (R4-2) as dominated by stacked Oxygen Canisters under the B1 hold. Of the remaining three, the Oxygen Canister and Dampener Coil bear on *surviving* the Beacon survival window, and the Signal Anchor bears on *setting it up* (pre-activation recon, not in-finale survival — round-15/16 reframe, C.7), C.5.6a.)*
